## 2. 2026-10-26 Primary-Source Protocol & SDK Deep Audit (Stages 266–267 · 29 Zero-Overlap PRs)

### 2.1 x402 Foundation (`x402-foundation/x402#3941`, `#3594`, `#3573`, `#3550`, `#3540`, `PR-01..PR-04`)
1. **SVM Exact `SettlementResponse.payer` Wallet Authority Alignment (`#3941`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Aligns `specs/schemes/exact/scheme_exact_svm.md` `SettlementResponse.payer` description with the Go/TS/Python facilitator implementations: `"base58 encoded public address of the payer's wallet"` (the SPL token transfer authority at `transferIx.accounts[3]`), NOT the transaction fee payer / facilitator (`transactionMessage.feePayer` at `accountKeys[0]`).
2. **Cardano Masumi Escrow Address Callback Decoupling (`#3594`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Removes the direct pre-build import of `@x402/cardano` in `e2e/src/mechanisms.ts` by passing an optional callback `masumiEscrowAddress?: (network: string) => string` into `resolvePaymentRoutes` and `resolvePayTo`.
3. **Python `x402[svm]` `solana>=0.36.0,<0.40` Upper Bound (`#3573`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Caps `solana>=0.36.0,<0.40` in `python/x402/pyproject.toml` because `solana 0.40.0` removed `solana.rpc.api` (`Client`, `AsyncClient`), breaking `from x402.mechanisms.svm import KeypairSigner`.
4. **TypeScript Release Pipeline `verify:exports` Fail-Closed Guard & Post-Quantum Block-Height Proof (`#3550`, `#3540`, `PR-04`)**:
   - `typescript/scripts/verify-package-exports.mjs` runs `pnpm verify:exports` in release/publish workflows and fails closed (`process.exit(1)`) if `checkedCount === 0` (unless `VERIFY_EXPORTS_ALLOW_EMPTY=1`) or if any declared `exports` target file (`.d.ts`/`.d.cts`/`.mjs`/`.cjs`) is missing; also audited `socseal-verify` (`#3540`) requiring an `ML-DSA-87` (FIPS-204) signature receipt over confirmed block height (`height > 0`).

### 2.2 Google A2A Python SDK (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1377`, `#1375`, `#1286`, `#1277`, `#1274`, `#1273`, `PR-05..PR-07`)
1. **REST Tenant Routes Mounted Under `{path_prefix}/{tenant}` (`#1377`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Fixes `create_rest_routes` mounting tenant routes at `f'{path_prefix}/{{tenant}}'` with unprefixed `tenant_route_objects` so requests to `/a2a/rest/my-tenant/message:send` resolve `context.tenant == 'my-tenant'` instead of 404ing under `/{tenant}/a2a/rest/...`.
2. **Malformed JSON-RPC Envelope Guard & Extra-Field Error Mapping (`#1375`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Replaces raw `JSONRPC20Response(**response_data)` in `JsonRpcTransport._check_jsonrpc_response` and `_handle_sse_error` so non-dict JSON bodies, objects missing both `result` and `error`, or non-int `error.code` raise `A2AClientError`, while error objects carrying extra fields (e.g., `reason`) still map via `_ERROR_CODE_MAP` (such as `-32001` $\to$ `TaskNotFoundError`), including on `delete_task_push_notification_config`.
3. **Cross-SDK AgentCard JWS Interop Vectors, `application/a2a+json` Media Type & `ignore_unknown_fields` (`#1286`, `#1277`, `#1274`, `#1273`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Adds `signing_interop_vectors.json` (`a2a-card-sign-v01`) verifying identical canonical UTF-8 bytes and `ES256` signatures across `a2a-sdk 1.2.1`, `@a2a-js/sdk 1.3.0`, `a2a-go`, and a reference signer (`S0-001..S0-004` `MUST-ACCEPT`, `S0-REJECT-005` `MUST-REJECT`, `#1286`);
   - Labels HTTP+JSON REST responses with `Content-Type: application/a2a+json` via `A2AJSONResponse` (`#1274`);
   - Enables `ignore_unknown_fields=True` in JSON-RPC and REST protobuf parsing while returning `-32602` with a typed `google.rpc.ErrorInfo` array on structural type mismatches (`#1273`), and preserves `TaskStatus.timestamp`, `icon_url`, `oauth2_metadata_url`, and `skill.security` in `v0_3/proto_utils.py` (`#1277`).

### 2.3 Stripe CLI & Multi-Language SDKs (`stripe-cli#2155`, `#2143`, `#2141`, `stripe-node#2895`, `stripe-go#2457`, `PR-08..PR-09`)
1. **Stripe CLI Major Plugin Auto-Upgrade Block, OS/Arch Build Diagnostics & macOS Signing Secrets (`#2155`, `#2143`, `#2141`, `PR-08`)**:
   - `isMajorPluginUpgrade` skips silent `maybeAutoUpgrade` across major semver boundaries (`1.x` $\to$ `2.x`) and warns on `stderr` to run `stripe plugin upgrade <name>` (`#2141`);
   - `explainPinnedVersionNotFound` performs an unpinned metadata lookup on 404 to distinguish missing OS/arch builds from nonexistent versions (`#2143`);
   - `release.yml` passes all 5 `MACOS_SIGN_*` and `MACOS_NOTARY_*` secrets to GoReleaser (`#2155`).
2. **Stripe Node Discarded Fetch Retry Disposal / Async Webhook Verify & Stripe Go Empty Page Guard (`stripe-node#2895`, `stripe-go#2457`, `PR-09`)**:
   - `stripe-node#2895` calls `res.dispose?.()` before retrying a failed `fetch` request (aborting the signal and canceling/destroying the unread response stream) and awaits `parseEventNotificationAsync(...)` for Web Crypto compatibility;
   - `stripe-go#2457` validates V1 list pages via `validateV1ListPage`, returning `errEmptyPageWithHasMore` (`stripe: invalid list response: has_more is true but data is empty`) when `len(data) == 0 && has_more && !single`.

### 2.4 Coinbase AgentKit (`coinbase/agentkit#1510`, `#1508`, `#1504`, `PR-10`)
1. **`x402Guard` Pre-Payment EIP-712 Seller Trust Census & `KeeperHub` `taskId` Idempotent Replay (`#1510`, `#1504`, `PR-10`)**:
   - `x402GuardActionProvider` (`#1510`) queries EIP-712 signed Bazaar endpoint verdicts (`pay` / `caution` / `avoid`) prior to `x402_http_request` and blocks `avoid` endpoints;
   - `KeeperHubActionProvider` (`#1504`) enforces `simulate: true` before broadcast and derives an idempotency key from `taskId` + transfer fields so lost-receipt timeouts replay the existing `executionId` instead of double-spending (`0/100` duplicate transfers vs. `47/47` on raw `erc20.transfer`).

### 2.5 Model Context Protocol Official SDKs & Ext-Apps (`PR-11..PR-16`)
1. **MCP Python SDK Strict Token Expiry Boundary, Non-Integer Probe IDs & 300s Read Timeout (`python-sdk#3761`, `#3625`, `#3619`, `#3618`, `PR-11..PR-12`)**:
   - Uses strict `time.time() < self._expiry` in `identity_assertion.py` and `oauth2.py` (`#3761`) so tokens at `expires_in=0` or `now == expiry` are treated as expired per RFC 7519 §4.1.4;
   - Uses non-integer `listen-1` request IDs for internal stream probes (`#3619`), configures `httpx2.Timeout(30.0, read=300.0)` (`#3618`), and documents shielded cancellation cleanup and `stateless_http=True`/`json_response=True` caveats (`#3625`).
2. **MCP Go / Rust / Java / C# SDKs & Ext-Apps v2 Role-Peer Isolation (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - `go-sdk#1293`/`#1292`/`#1290` (`PR-13`): extends `http.Server` write deadlines by `2 * s.keepAliveInterval` on each stream write, permits `AddReceivingCustomMethod` over SSE transport, and normalizes `nil` completion/prompt slices to `[]`;
   - `rust-sdk#1285` (`PR-14`): calls `credential_store.on_refresh_token_rejected(&stored_credentials)` under the refresh guard solely on `invalid_grant`, preserving credentials on transient errors;
   - `java-sdk#1210`/`#1201` (`PR-15`): invokes per-subscription `notifierFactory` in `Flux.defer` (rejecting `null` with `INTERNAL_ERROR`) and coerces non-string `LoggingMessageNotification.data` via `.toString()`;
   - `csharp-sdk#1878`/`#1874` & `ext-apps#720` (`PR-16`): updates `ui/initialize` to `protocolVersion: "2026-01-26"`, preserves `MarshalResult` in `McpServerToolCreateOptions.Clone()`, and isolates `@modelcontextprotocol/client` and `@modelcontextprotocol/server` optional role peers.

## 4. Stages 268–269 Incremental Deep Dive: x402 Next/Axios Adapters, A2A Weak ETag/Context/Media, Stripe Webhook/CLI/Python, Coinbase AgentKit & MCP Multi-SDK Sweep/Origin/CLI Guards (29 Zero-Overlap PRs)

### 4.1 x402 Foundation (`x402-foundation/x402#3456`, `#3454`, `#3451`, `#3387`, `PR-01..PR-02`)
1. **`@x402/next` Leading Empty Query Value Preservation & `req.clone().json()` Body Stream Protection (`#3456`, `#3451`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Replaces truthy `if (existing)` with `if (existing !== undefined)` in `NextAdapter.getQueryParams()` so `?tag=&tag=b` preserves `["", "b"]` instead of dropping the leading empty string;
   - Reads JSON bodies via `await this.req.clone().json()` in `NextAdapter.getBody()` so downstream route handlers can still read `request.json()`.
2. **`@x402/axios` `getUri(config)` Resolution & Post-Signature Timeout Non-Repayment (`#3454`, `#3387`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Uses `new URL(axiosInstance.getUri(config)).href` so `baseURL` path segments and `config.params` query parameters are preserved in hook contexts;
   - Prohibits automatic re-payment when a request carrying `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` times out or returns `settlement_pending` (`#3387`), requiring idempotent reconciliation first.

### 4.2 Google A2A Python & Java SDKs (`a2a-python#1272`, `#1270`, `#1269`, `#1268` & `a2a-java#1200`, `#1198`, `PR-03..PR-05`)
1. **A2A AgentCard Signature-Stripped Weak `ETag` (`W/"..."`) & RFC 9110 `304 Not Modified` (`a2a-python#1272`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Strips `signatures` before hashing `json.dumps(unsigned, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))` into a weak `ETag` (`W/"<sha256>"`) so randomized `ECDSA_SHA_256` signatures do not invalidate caches, and evaluates RFC 9110 §8.2.2 weak `If-None-Match` (`304 Not Modified`).
2. **A2A Pre-Execution `contextId` Task Binding & Terminal `UnsupportedOperationError` (`a2a-python#1270`, `#1268`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Rejects `message.context_id != task.context_id` with `InvalidParamsError` before `executor.execute` runs, and raises `UnsupportedOperationError` (`TaskNotCancelableError` on cancel) for operations against terminal tasks (`COMPLETED`/`CANCELED`/`FAILED`/`REJECTED`).
3. **A2A `validate_input_modes` Decorator, Java REST `%d` Query Formatting & JSON-RPC `"id": null` Serialization (`a2a-python#1269` & `a2a-java#1200`, `#1198`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Enforces `default_input_modes ∪ skill.input_modes` via `@validate_input_modes` (`ContentTypeNotSupportedError`); fixes `/tasks/%s?historyLength=%d` in `a2a-java#1198`; and forces `setSerializeNulls(true)` in `JsonUtil.writeJsonRpcId` (`a2a-java#1200`) so `"id": null` is always written.

### 4.3 Stripe Node / Python / CLI (`stripe-node#2885`, `#2876`, `stripe-python#1920`, `#1911`, `stripe-cli#2134`, `#2125`, `#2124`, `PR-06..PR-08`)
1. **Stripe Node Blank Webhook Secret Rejection & `verifyHeader` Nullish Tolerance (`stripe-node#2885`, `#2876`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Rejects ASCII-whitespace-only webhook secrets via `isBlankWebhookSecret` (`StripeSignatureVerificationError`) and applies `tolerance ?? Webhook.DEFAULT_TOLERANCE` (300s default, `0` skips check).
2. **Stripe Python Event Context Callback Account Isolation & Positional-Only Path Params (`stripe-python#1920`, `#1911`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Prevents `StripeClient.with_stripe_context` from leaking the parent client's `stripe_account` into `parse_event_notification` callbacks (`received_account is None`), and enforces positional-only (`/`) resource path parameters.
3. **Stripe CLI Dot/Empty Segment Rejection, Positional ID `url.PathEscape` & Explicit `HookBaseURLs` (`stripe-cli#2134`, `#2125`, `#2124`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Rejects `""`, `"."`, and `".."` in `EscapePathSegment`, applies `url.PathEscape` to positional resource IDs in `formatURL`, and forwards only `explicitFlagValue` `HookBaseURLs` during plugin `Install`/`Upgrade`.

### 4.4 Coinbase AgentKit (`coinbase/agentkit#1487`, `#1491`, `#1492`, `PR-09`)
1. **Dexter Built-in Facilitator, WalletForge Base USDC Caps & HCRB Chain Guard (`PR-09`)**:
   - Registers `dexter` (`https://x402.dexter.cash`) in `KNOWN_FACILITATORS` (`#1487`), enforces `WalletForgeActionProvider` Base USDC caps (`0.05`/`0.01` USDC, `#1491`), and restricts `HcrbActionProvider` to Base (`8453`) / Base Sepolia (`84532`, `#1492`).

### 4.5 MCP Go / Rust / Java / C# / Inspector (`PR-10..PR-16`)
1. **MCP Go Present-Empty `Mcp-Param-*` Header Check, Generation-Tagged Subscriptions, Stdio Batch `IsCall()` Filtering & Version Membership (`go-sdk#1289`, `#1283`, `#1277`, `#1268`, `PR-10..PR-11`)**:
   - Distinguishes present-empty headers (`len(header.Values(h)) > 0`) from absent headers (`#1289`), tags `ClientSession.resourceSubs` with monotonic `gen uint64` (`#1283`), tracks only `req.IsCall()` items in stdio batches (`#1277`), and validates `_meta.protocolVersion` by membership in `SupportedProtocolVersions()` (`#1268`).
2. **MCP Rust `Origin` RFC 6454 Default Port Matching, Initialize-less First-Request Loop Dispatch & Cancel Routing (`rust-sdk#1270`, `#1263`, `#1262`, `PR-12..PR-13`)**:
   - Normalizes omitted `Origin` ports to scheme defaults (`80`/`443`, `#1270`), seeds `batch_messages` with `initial_messages` inside `serve_inner` (`#1263`), and routes `notifications/cancelled` via `is_subscription` (`#1262`).
3. **MCP Java Idle Session Sweep & Error Sanitization, MCP C# MRTR Dispose Race & Doc Symlinks, MCP Inspector CLI Conflict & Auto-Task Routing (`java-sdk#1185`/`#1184`, `csharp-sdk#1860`/`#1856`, `inspector#2701`/`#2699`, `PR-14..PR-16`)**:
   - Evicts idle streamless Java sessions on `sessionSweepInterval` (`#1185`) and strips internal cause chains from `JSONRPCError.data` (`#1184`); cancels `_serverLifetimeCts` before sweeping `_mrtrContinuations` with publish-then-recheck in C# (`#1860`) and rejects doc symlinks (`#1856`); rejects conflicting CLI target/URL/stdio flags in Inspector (`#2701`) and auto-routes `taskSupport == "required"` tools via `callToolStream` (`#2699`).
4. **Stages 268–269 Engineering Assets & Verification**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_guards.py)
   - Benchmark suite: [`lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_bench.py)
   - Unit tests: [`tests/test_x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep.py`](../../tests/test_x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep.py) (**`952/952` unit tests passing — 950+ unit test milestone achieved!**)
   - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-next-axios-a2a-etag-stripe-webhook-agentkit-mcp-sweep-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-next-axios-a2a-etag-stripe-webhook-agentkit-mcp-sweep-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-next-axios-a2a-etag-stripe-webhook-agentkit-mcp-sweep-bench/report.json).

## 5. Stages 270–271 Incremental Deep-Dive Audit (53 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs, `PR-01..PR-16` · 270-Stage Milestone & `956/956` Unit Tests Passing!)

### 5.1 x402 Foundation (`x402-foundation/x402#3278`, `#3275`, `#3251`, `#3240`, `PR-01..PR-02`)
1. **Facilitator `EXTENSION-RESPONSES` Server Sidechannel Isolation & Canonical `x402UptoPermit2Proxy` CREATE2 Restore (`#3278`, `#3275`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Decodes facilitator `EXTENSION-RESPONSES` into `VerifyResponse.extensionResponses` / `SettleResponse.extensionResponses` without merging into buyer-visible `extensions`, and strips `extensionResponses` before encoding `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` (`#3278`);
   - Restores canonical `x402UptoPermit2Proxy` CREATE2 address `0x4020A4f3b7b90ccA423B9fabCc0CE57C6C240002` by reverting the accidental `ReentrancyGuardTransient` bytecode change (`#3275`).
2. **Batch-Settlement Client Local Channel Derivation & Upfront Exact Flow (`#3251`, `#3240`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Computes client-local channel state via `local_cumulative + min(chargedAmount, maxAllowed) + clientSignedDeposit`, skips writing storage if a present `chargedCumulativeAmount` mismatches, caps refunds to locally refundable balance (`#3251`), and supports upfront `exact` payment without a preliminary 402 round-trip (`#3240`).

### 5.2 Google A2A Go / Java / .NET / JS / Python SDKs (`PR-03..PR-05`)
1. **A2A Go `Accept` Negotiation, UTC `.sssZ` Timestamps & Terminal `ErrUnsupportedOperation` (`a2a-go#465`, `#452`, `#442`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Selects `application/json` only when `Accept` explicitly requests `application/json` without `application/a2a+json` (`#465`), formats `TaskStatus.Timestamp` in UTC with `.sssZ` millisecond precision (`#452`), and returns `ErrUnsupportedOperation` on `SendMessage`/`SubscribeToTask` for terminal stored tasks (`#442`).
2. **A2A Java Atomic Single Terminal SSE Callback, Blank ID Normalization & RFC 3986 Redirects (`a2a-java#1181`, `#1179`, `#1174`, `#1173`, `#1172`, `#1162`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Uses a shared `AtomicBoolean.compareAndSet(false, true)` in `AbstractSSEEventListener` so at most one terminal callback fires (`#1173`), normalizes blank `""` `taskId`/`contextId` to `null` (`#1172`), resolves relative redirect `Location` headers against the request URI (`#1162`), and covers `A2ACardResolver` fallback URL (`#1179`), gRPC explicit JSON nulls (`#1174`), and REST `application/a2a+json` (`#1181`).
3. **A2A .NET Parse Error Sanitization, JS v0.3 Push Compat & Python Integer Nanosecond Ordering (`a2a-dotnet#452`, `a2a-js#737`/`#726`, `a2a-python#1233`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Sanitizes `JsonException` parse errors to static `"Invalid JSON payload"` (`CWE-209`, `a2a-dotnet#452`), converts v0.3 push notifications to v1.0 `StreamResponse` (`a2a-js#726`) with configurable store table names (`a2a-js#737`), and orders `InMemoryTaskStore.list()` timestamps via `ToNanoseconds()` integers (`a2a-python#1233`).

### 5.3 Stripe Node / Python / CLI (`stripe-node#2888`/`#2868`/`#2867`, `stripe-python#1904`/`#1902`/`#1898`, `stripe-cli#2128`/`#2126`/`#2116`, `PR-06..PR-08`)
1. **Stripe Node Truncated Body `StripeConnectionError`, Immediate Interceptor Write & Constructable ESM (`#2888`, `#2868`, `#2867`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Classifies mid-stream severed buffered bodies as retryable `StripeConnectionError` (`#2868`), writes/ends `NodeHttpClient` requests immediately without waiting for `socket` so Nock 14 / MSW interceptors do not hang (`#2867`), and exports a constructable `Stripe` class in `stripe.esm.node.ts` (`#2888`).
2. **Stripe Python 30-Byte CSPRNG Multipart Boundary, Header Escaping & Singleton `RelatedObject` (`#1904`, `#1902`, `#1898`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Generates 60-hex CSPRNG boundaries via `secrets.token_hex(30)` and escapes `"` (`%22`), `\r`, and `\n` (`" "`) in `Content-Disposition` `name`/`filename` headers (`#1898`), supports `id=None` singleton `EventNotification` related objects (`#1904`), and validates non-empty webhook secrets (`#1902`).
3. **Stripe CLI `-p` Profile OAuth Active Context Sync & Windows Binary Rename Retry (`#2128`, `#2126`, `#2116`, `PR-08`)**:

## 6. Stages 272–273 Incremental Deep-Dive Audit (86 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs, `PR-01..PR-16` · `960/960` Unit Tests Passing!)

### 6.1 x402 Foundation & Universal Commerce Protocol (`PR-01..PR-04`)
1. **x402 Offline-Verifiable `authority` Mandate, Request-Bound `payment-identifier` (`409 Conflict`) & SIWX Origin Binding (`x402#3220`, `#3218`, `#3192`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Verifies signed offline `authority` spending mandates (`mandate_delegate == caller_identity` and `requested_amount <= mandate_max_amount`, closing the gap between payment verification and caller spending authorization, `#3220`), binds `payment-identifier` cache entries to the HTTP request fingerprint `(method, path, bodySha256)` returning `409 Conflict` on payload mutation or concurrent in-flight collision (`#3218`), and binds Python SIWX client challenges to the target request origin (`#3192`).
2. **x402 Bazaar Fixed-Point `routeTemplate` Decode, `settlement_pending` Auto-Recovery & Exact-Hedera `transferExecutor` (`x402#3213`, `#3214`, `#3205`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Decodes `routeTemplate` via iterative fixed-point `decodeURIComponent` before checking for `".."` traversal and `"://"` scheme injection (`#3213`), introduces `settlement_pending` auto-recovery for broadcast-but-unconfirmed transactions (`#3214`), and validates Exact-Hedera `transferExecutor` allowance/recipient semantics (`#3205`).
3. **x402 Python Facilitator Configurable `gas_limit`, Family-Scoped Signers, Sei Default USDC & `auth-capture` v1.1 (`x402#3233`, `#3228`, `#3227`, `#3203`, `#3197`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Adds constructor-level `gas_limit` to Python `FacilitatorWeb3Signer` (`#3233`), scopes `createE2EClient()` EVM/SVM signer derivation to selected `--families` (`#3228`), adds Sei Mainnet (`eip155:1329`) and Testnet (`eip155:1328`) native USDC defaults across TS/Go/Python SDKs (`#3227`), and enforces `auth-capture` v1.1 authorization hold and partial capture (`captureAmount <= authorizedMax`) invariants (`#3203`, `#3197`).
4. **UCP Nested Shopping Extensions, Root Link `#fragment` Preservation, `ucp-schema` Fail-Fast, `llms.txt` Indexing, `line_item.note` & Catalog Envelope Reference (`ucp#761`, `#759`, `#726`, `#720`, `#607`, `#465`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Reorganizes shopping extensions under `docs/specification/shopping/extensions/` with redirects (`#761`), preserves `#fragment` anchors when rewriting root spec links (`#759`), fails fast when `ucp-schema` or `cargo` are missing before `sed` pipelines (`#726`), indexes `split-payments.md` and `glossary.md` in `llms.txt` (`#720`), enforces `line_item.note` (`maxLength: 500`, `#607`), and renders `response_catalog_schema` metadata on the schema reference page (`#465`).

### 6.2 Google A2A Python / Go / JS / Java / .NET SDKs (`PR-05..PR-08`)
1. **A2A Python `TaskUpdater` Validation Recovery, v0.3 Unset `None`, AgentCard JCS Signing Presence, Per-Client `httpx` & Lazy Cluster Tables (`a2a-python#1365`, `#1362`, `#1360`, `#1358`, `#1357`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Validates status events before latching the terminal flag in `TaskUpdater.update_status()` (`#1365`), checks `HasField` in v0.3 `FromProto` so unset `status.update` and `card.provider` stay `None` (`#1362`), preserves explicit default/empty fields on `AgentCard` before JCS signing (`#1360`), creates a dedicated `httpx` client per `ClientFactory` client (`#1358`), and lazily initializes cluster tables on `VersionedDatabaseTaskStore.get`/`delete` (`#1357`).
2. **A2A Go REST Endpoint Query Preservation, Lowercase `ServiceParams`, Nil `Typed.MarshalJSON`, Non-A2A HTTP Status Mapping, `a2a+json` Negotiation & Pre-Stream Error (`a2a-go#480`, `#479`, `#474`, `#471`, `#468`, `#463`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Preserves existing query parameters (e.g. `?api_key=...`) on advertised REST endpoints (`#480`), normalizes `ServiceParams` keys to lowercase (`#479`), guards `nil` `Value` maps in `Typed.MarshalJSON` (`#474`), maps non-A2A gateway/proxy HTTP error responses by status code (`#471`), honors `Content-Type: application/a2a+json` when `Accept` is `*/*` or omitted (`#468`), and returns plain errors before sending `200 text/event-stream` headers when streaming fails prior to the first event (`#463`).
3. **A2A JS gRPC First-Event Extensions, Typed SSE Errors, Sub-ms `statusTimestampAfter`, Unknown Payload Skip & Java REST SSE Completion/Close (`a2a-js#791`, `#788`, `#787`, `#770` & `a2a-java#1209`, `#1202`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Defers gRPC response metadata until the first streamed event so extensions activated during initial execution are included (`a2a-js#791`), throws typed A2A errors from JSON-RPC SSE error events (`#788`), preserves sub-millisecond fractional precision in `statusTimestampAfter` comparisons (`#787`), skips unrecognized stream payload kinds (`#770`), signals normal Java REST SSE stream completion (`a2a-java#1209`), and cancels in-flight HTTP streaming futures on `RestTransport.close()` (`#1202`).
4. **A2A .NET Proactive `ContinueTaskAsync`, Consolidated `IA2ARequestHandler`, Non-Event-Stream Error, Registry Extension Negotiation & Duplicate JSON Key Rejection (`a2a-dotnet#554`, `#553`, `#552`, `#548`, `#546`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Adds `A2AServer.ContinueTaskAsync` without synthetic inbound messages (`#554`), consolidates context-aware `IA2ARequestHandler` (`#553`), raises an error when a streaming call is not answered with `text/event-stream` (`#552`), implements registry-backed extension negotiation (`#548`), and recursively rejects duplicate JSON-RPC object keys before deserialization (`#546`).

## 7. Stages 274–275 Incremental Deep-Dive Audit (137 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs, `PR-01..PR-16` · 275-Stage Milestone & `964/964` Unit Tests Passing!)

### 7.1 x402 Foundation & Universal Commerce Protocol (`PR-01..PR-04`)
1. **x402 TS SVM `exact` `TransferChecked` Discriminator (`data[0] === 12`), TS SIWX Origin Binding, Monad USDC Domain & Null `accepted` Guard (`x402#3132`, `#3133`, `#3153`, `#3180`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Enforces `data[0] === 12` (`TransferChecked` discriminator) at instruction index 2 on the TS SVM `exact` static path (`#3132`), binds TS SIWX client challenges to the 402 response origin before signing (`#3133`), aligns Monad USDC v1 EIP-712 `domain.name` to `"USDC"` (`#3153`), and handles missing/null `accepted` requirements without throwing a runtime `TypeError` (`#3180`).
2. **x402 Go `spendControls`, TS SVM `upto` `ComputeBudget` Instructions, SVM Batch Settlement & Injected RPC (`x402#3156`, `#3155`, `#3164`, `#3183`, `#3190`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Ports buyer `spendControls` (`maxAmountPerCall`, `maxCumulativeSpend`, `allowedPayees`) to the Go SDK (`#3156`), adds `ComputeBudget` instructions to TS SVM `upto` transactions (`#3155`), implements the SVM `batch-settlement` scheme (`#3164`), and supports injecting an RPC client into Go/TS SVM `upto` facilitators (`#3183`, `#3190`).
3. **x402 `offer-receipt` v2 Proof-of-Delivery `responseHash`, Experimental `shared-payment-token` (`spt`) & `builder-code` `a` Validation (`x402#3186`, `#3131`, `#3241`, `#3255`, `#3274`, `#3315`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Binds delivered response bytes to `offer-receipt` v2 via signed `responseHash`, `responseHashAlg`, and `responseHashEncoding` (`#3186`), verifies provider-neutral `shared-payment-token` (`spt`) fixed-price scope (`#3131`), aligns Go/Python/TS default assets and MCP payment flow hooks (`#3241`, `#3255`, `#3274`), and validates the `builder-code` `a` field (`#3315`).
4. **UCP Off-Site Link Classification in `check_links.py`, Serialized `gh-pages` Timeout & `uv` Pre-Commit (`ucp#889`, `#888`, `#886`, `#885`, `#878`, `#851`, `#772`, `#704`, `#700`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Treats protocol-relative (`//...`) and off-site links as external in `check_links.py` (`#772`), serializes `gh-pages` deployments with job timeouts (`#700`), runs pre-commit validators through `uv` (`#704`), and keeps latest spec links and release backports synchronized (`#851`, `#886`, `#885`, `#878`, `#888`, `#889`).

### 7.2 Google A2A Specification & Python / Java / JS SDKs (`PR-05..PR-08`)
1. **A2A Client-Written `artifacts`, Non-Terminal Interrupted `TaskState` Semantics & `jcs_depth_v1` Bound (`A2A#2322`, `#2319`, `#2310`, `#2270`, `#2246`, `#2240`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Allows clients to write artifacts via `SendMessageRequest` (`#2322`), clarifies that `TASK_STATE_INPUT_REQUIRED` and `TASK_STATE_AUTH_REQUIRED` are non-terminal interrupted states (`#2270`), pins the RFC 8785 JCS nesting bound (`<= 128`) with the `jcs_depth_v1` corpus (`#2246`), and updates §8.4.1 AgentCard canonicalization, `Message.sender`, and AI Catalog discovery (`#2319`, `#2310`, `#2240`).
2. **A2A Python Push Webhook SSRF Hardening, Deep-Copied Push Config Store & `A2A-Version` Minor Check (`a2a-python#1164`, `#1218`, `#1220`, `#1222`, `#1225`, `#1243`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Validates push-notification webhook URLs before dispatch to block SSRF against `169.254.169.254`, loopback, and private networks (`#1164`), deep-copies `InMemoryPushNotificationConfigStore` entries so callers cannot mutate stored webhooks (`#1218`), avoids out-of-range datetime ordering crashes (`#1220`), configures SSE shutdown grace periods (`#1222`), and validates `A2A-Version` minor versions (`#1225`, `#1243`).
3. **A2A Python Terminal Escape Block, Internal Error Sanitization & `history_length` Cap (`a2a-python#1183`, `#1182`, `#1180`, `#1177`, `#1176`, `#1167`, `#1165`, `#1158`, `#1153`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Blocks tasks in terminal states (`COMPLETED`/`FAILED`/`CANCELED`/`REJECTED`) from transitioning back to `WORKING` (`#1182`), sanitizes internal exception messages in JSON-RPC and gRPC paths (`#1183`), enforces an upper bound on `history_length` (`#1177`), validates task ID length and message content (`#1176`), and enqueues `Task` before `TaskStatusUpdateEvent` in TCK (`#1165`).
4. **A2A Java `PostBuilder` Redirect Default=False, 1 MB SSE Line Cap, Per-Task Push Order & Tenant `404` (`a2a-java#1141`, `#1148`, `#1149`, `#1156`, `#1163`, `#1124`, `#1112` & `a2a-js#743`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Defaults `PostBuilder.followRedirects` to `false` across JDK, Vert.x, and Android clients to prevent cross-origin credential leakage (`#1141`), adds `SSEParserConfig` with a 1 MB per-line limit (`#1149`), preserves per-task push notification order (`#1156`), returns `404` for unknown tenants on public agent cards (`#1112`, `#1124`), and incorporates `GHSA-4fpj-g9jf-9rgg` into the threat model (`#1148`).

   - Uses exact string-backed decimals in Go (`#2442`, `#2455`), represents free-form objects as dynamic maps (`#2440`, `#2451`), drops Go 1.22/1.23 (`#2453`), preserves custom install directories during CLI auto-updates (`#2111`), and unifies `agentsetup` `RunCommand` (`#2115`, `#2118`).
4. **Coinbase AgentKit ERC-20 Metadata Prompt-Injection Sanitization & x402 Quote Binding (`agentkit#1480`, `#1468`, `#1481`, `#1479`, `#1474`, `#1473`, `#1464`, `#1462`, `PR-12`)**:
   - Sanitizes untrusted ERC-20 token `name`/`symbol` metadata against indirect prompt injection (`#1480`), binds x402 two-step quotes to settlement identity (`payTo`) and `maxPaymentUsdc` (`#1468`), and covers `merona` seller checks (`#1464`), `TruthBear` (`#1473`), `DefiLlama` (`#1479`), and `Taskmarket` (`#1462`).

### 7.4 MCP 7-Language SDKs (Go / Rust / Python / TypeScript / C# / Java / Kotlin) & Inspector (`PR-13..PR-16`)
1. **MCP Go Legacy Body Limit, CORS No-`OPTIONS`, `json.Number` Precision & Rust Saturating SSE Backoff (`go-sdk#1223`, `#1224`, `#1226`, `#1232`, `#1241`, `#1244`, `#1339`, `#1409` & `rust-sdk#1231`, `#1247`, `#1256`, `PR-13`)**:
   - Enforces a max request body size on the Go legacy SSE transport (`#1224`), adds `StreamKeepAlive` SSE comments (`#1232`), preserves JSON number precision in tool schemas (`#1244`), forbids `OPTIONS` in CORS `AllowedMethods` (`#1223`), saturates Rust exponential SSE reconnect backoff to prevent overflow panics (`#1231`), exposes protocol version negotiation (`#1247`), and stops keeping a session/GET stream on `2026-07-28` (`#1256`).
2. **MCP Python Exact OAuth `resource` Indicator, Replay Buffer & TypeScript Separate `Set-Cookie` Forwarding (`python-sdk#3462`, `#3470`, `#3485`, `#3516`, `#3541` & `typescript-sdk#2990`, `#2993`, `#2994`, `#3009`, `#3083`, `PR-14`)**:
   - Preserves path-less OAuth `resource` indicators byte-for-byte without appending a trailing `/` (`#3462`), buffers replay before network delivery (`#3541`), forwards multiple `Set-Cookie` headers separately in the TS Node adapter (`#2990`), clears stale stdio frames before restart (`#2993`), and expands multiple scalar URI variables with RFC 6570 operator semantics (`#2994`).
3. **MCP C# SSE POST Disposal & CIMD `none` Auth, Java 400 Malformed Body & Kotlin `SEP-986` Tool Validation (`csharp-sdk#1838`, `#1839`, `#1841`, `#1843`, `#1845`, `#1849`, `#1853`, `java-sdk#1168`, `#1170` & `kotlin-sdk#695`, `#696`, `#697`, `#706`, `PR-15`)**:
   - Disposes POST responses in C# `SseClientSessionTransport` (`#1841`), closes stdio input before waiting for exit (`#1838`), uses `none` token-endpoint auth for CIMD public clients (`#1845`), reports malformed JSON bodies as `400 INVALID_REQUEST` in Java (`#1168`), validates tool names per `SEP-986` in Kotlin (`#695`), adds `$schema` per `SEP-1613` (`#696`), and propagates `CancellationException` (`#706`).
4. **MCP Inspector Modern-Era `'Run as task'` Suppression, Universal `--version` & TUI `Esc` Filter Clear (`inspector#2687`, `#2688`, `#2689`, `#2693`, `#2695`, `#2711`, `#2729`, `#2741`, `PR-16`)**:
   - Hides `'Run as task'` on modern-era (`2026-07-28`) connections via `offersRunAsTask` per `SEP-2663` (`#2687`), prints `--version` / `-V` across every launcher mode (`#2695`), and clears active TUI list filters on `Esc` before exiting (`#2693`).
5. **Stages 274–275 Engineering Assets & Verification**:

## 8. Stages 276–277 Incremental Deep-Dive Audit (203 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs, `PR-01..PR-16` · `968/968` Unit Tests Passing!)

### 8.1 x402 Foundation & Universal Commerce Protocol & MCP Spec (`PR-01..PR-04`)
1. **x402 Starknet Payer-Scoped `Transfer` Check, TS `spendControls`, SVM Smart-Wallet `ComputeBudget` & Operator Limits, XRPL Decode Allowlist & EVM Revert vs. RPC Classification (`x402#3126`, `#3124`, `#3122`, `#3120`, `#3115`, `#3114`, `#3105`, `#3101`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Filters Starknet `exact` `Transfer` events to `from == payer` and requires exactly one match so fee-transfer `STRK` events from the paymaster are not mistaken for the buyer's payment (`#3124`); adds TypeScript pre-signing `spendControls` with a `$1 USD` default ceiling (`#3126`); enforces SVM smart-wallet `ComputeBudget` instructions and operator-configured `maxComputeUnitPriceMicrolamports` (`#3122`, `#3105`); restricts XRPL decoded `Payment` fields to an explicit allowlist with canonical byte round-trip check (`#3115`); and separates reverted EVM simulations (`invalid_exact_evm_payload_simulation_reverted`, non-retryable) from RPC transport failures (`simulation_rpc_failed`, retryable, `#3101`).
2. **x402 Hedera Consensus-Record Net Credit Check, MCP Failed-Settlement Result Withholding, Go `permit2` & Python `eip3009` Post-Settle `Transfer` Log Verification & `DOTALL` Route Guard (`x402#3067`, `#3061`, `#3060`, `#3058`, `#3055`, `#3053`, `#3036`, `#3033`, `#3032`, `#3031`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Verifies the payee's actual net credit in Hedera Mirror Node consensus records so custom HTS fees deducting from the transfer cannot pass when `receipt.status == SUCCESS` (`#3060`); withholds MCP tool results in `createPaymentWrapper` when `SettleResponse.success === false` (`#3061`); verifies post-settle ERC-20 `Transfer` logs in Go `permit2` (`exact` + `upto`) and Python `exact/eip3009` (`#3058`, `#3036`); compiles wildcard route regexes with `re.DOTALL` / `[^/]` so embedded `\n`/`\r` line terminators cannot bypass paywalls (`#3053`, `#3031`); and re-runs approval/spend hooks before signing corrective 402 recovery payments in `callToolWithPayment` (`#3033`).
3. **x402 `builder-code` Server/Client Merge (`<= 5`), Go Hook Error Propagation & `512 KB` Body Cap, JSON 402 `no-store`, Exact Go Override Math & SVM SIWx Small-Order Key Rejection (`x402#3027`, `#3026`, `#3022`, `#2987`, `#2985`, `#2984`, `#2981`, `#2974`, `#2964`, `#2962`, `#2957`, `#2933`, `#2923`, `#2917`, `#2914`, `#2912`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Merges server and client `builder-code` `s` arrays with a 5-entry cap (`MAX_SERVICE_CODES = 5`, `#3027`); propagates Go payment-response hook errors, closes response bodies, and caps HTTP response reads at `512 KB` (`#3026`, `#2984`); mirrors `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` in JSON 402 bodies with `Cache-Control: no-store` (`#2981`); fails closed on unmatched network filters (`#2987`); computes Go dollar/percent settlement overrides with arbitrary-precision integer arithmetic (`#2962`); rejects small-order Ed25519 public keys in SVM SIWx (`#2923`); and preserves Go retry request bodies while propagating batch-settlement storage errors (`#2912`, `#2914`).
4. **UCP Fact-Accuracy Reference Audit, Food TC Announcement & MCP Protected Workflow Environment Isolation (`ucp#874`, `#734`, `#683` & `modelcontextprotocol#3426`, `#3406`, `#3404`, `#3390`, `#3386`, `#3385`, `#3383`, `#3380`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Verifies the UCP fact-accuracy reference audit (`#874`), Food TC announcement (`#734`), and domain carousel assets (`#683`), while isolating MCP SEP automation and slash-command jobs inside protected GitHub deployment environments (`#3383`) and auditing the Infrastructure WG charter, Financial Services IG links, `mcpc` CLI matrix, and homepage security policy visibility (`#3426`, `#3406`, `#3404`, `#3390`, `#3386`, `#3385`, `#3380`).

### 8.2 Google A2A Specification & Python / Java / JS SDKs (`PR-05..PR-07`)
1. **A2A `v1.0.1` §5.4 Error-Mapping Table 6-Cell Wire Fix, `jcs_edge_v1` Vectors & ACTS Grammar (`A2A#2237`, `#2232`, `#2230`, `#2227`, `#2226`, `#2225`, `#2224`, `#2223`, `#2222`, `#2219`, `#2205`, `#2204`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Corrects 6 HTTP/gRPC status cells in §5.4 (`MethodNotFoundError` HTTP `404`/`405` & gRPC `UNIMPLEMENTED`, `InvalidParamsError` HTTP `400` & `INVALID_ARGUMENT`, `InternalError` HTTP `500`, `InvalidAgentResponseError` HTTP `502`, `#2230`), adds the `jcs_edge_v1` RFC 8785 edge-case corpus rejecting lone surrogates and normalizing `-0.0` (`#2237`), and documents A2A (`AgentCard.securitySchemes`) vs. MCP (`401` + OAuth PRM) authorization discovery (`#2204`).
2. **A2A Python Multi-Scheme `AND` Security Requirement Semantics, REST Path Sanitization, Authenticated Push Notifications & Full-Queue Tap Eviction (`a2a-python#1144`, `#1143`, `#1142`, `#1140`, `#1139`, `#1137`, `#1127`, `#1126`, `#1125`, `#1116`, `#1115`, `#1114`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Enforces `AND` semantics across all schemes within a single security requirement object (`#1144`), sanitizes REST path parameters (`task_id`, `push_id`) against null bytes, CR/LF, slashes, and `..` (`#1143`), attaches `Authorization` headers on push notifications when `PushNotificationConfig.authentication` is set (`#1139`), evicts full subscriber taps in `EventQueueSource._dispatch_loop` (`#1137`), and restores `_call_context` while awaiting cancelled producer tasks in `ActiveTask.cancel()` (`#1126`, `#1125`).
3. **A2A Java Fail-Closed Task Authorization, `AuthInterceptor` Header Allowlist/CRLF/Redirect Guard (`GHSA-9rhm-2h4x-jwmx`) & Mapped-IPv6 SSRF Block (`a2a-java#1109`, `#1104`, `#1099`, `#1098`, `#1097`, `#1096`, `#1095`, `#1090`, `#1088`, `#1085` & `a2a-js#781`, `#779`, `#702`, `#693`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Enforces fail-closed default task authorization in `DefaultRequestHandler` (`#1109`), hardens `AuthInterceptor` with a safe header allowlist, CRLF rejection, and disabled HTTP redirects (`#1104`), blocks private/loopback/link-local and IPv4-mapped IPv6 (`::ffff:127.0.0.1`, `::ffff:169.254.169.254`) in `PushNotificationUrlValidator` (`#1099`), and adds multitenant `beans.xml` CDI discovery plus JS dependency security updates (`#1097`, `a2a-js#781`, `#779`, `#702`, `#693`).

### 8.3 Stripe Node / Python / Go / CLI & Coinbase AgentKit (`PR-08..PR-11`)
1. **Stripe Node-AWS Workload Identity Pilot, `Stripe.V2List<T>` V1 Field Removal & Callback Client Context Isolation (`stripe-node#2859`, `#2858`, `#2857`, `#2856`, `#2855`, `#2854`, `#2853`, `#2850`, `#2846`, `#2845`, `#2844`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Ships the Workload Identities Node-AWS Pilot for short-lived token authentication without static API keys (`#2846`), removes legacy V1 fields (`object`, `has_more`, `url`) from `Stripe.V2List<T>` (`#2856`), fixes `EventNotificationHandler` callback client context isolation (`#2855`), and enforces `sdk-release/next-major` PR targets and `permissions: {}` workflow scope (`#2850`, `#2844`).
2. **Stripe Python `EventNotificationHandler` `.pre_handle` Hook & Async Event Handlers (`stripe-python#1894`, `#1893`, `#1892`, `#1888`, `#1887`, `#1885`, `#1881`, `#1879`, `#1873`, `#1871`, `#1869`, `#1868`, `PR-09`)**:
   - Adds `EventNotificationHandler` with a centralized `.pre_handle` hook for logging/deduplication before per-event handlers (`#1881`), supports async event handlers (`#1888`), and exposes `object == "event"` on `EventNotification` (`#1887`).
3. **Stripe Go `ConstructEvent` Empty Secret Rejection & Stripe CLI Space-Separated Booleans & 24h Upgrade Cooldown (`stripe-go#2439`, `#2438`, `#2437`, `#2436`, `#2435`, `#2432`, `#2431`, `#2429` & `stripe-cli#2109`, `#2108`, `#2107`, `#2106`, `#2105`, `#2104`, `#2103`, `#2102`, `#2095`, `#2093`, `#2092`, `PR-10`)**:
   - Rejects empty webhook signing secrets in Go `ConstructEvent` (`#2429`), parses space-separated boolean flags (`--disabled false`) in Stripe CLI (`#2105`), holds CLI upgrade notices until `published_at` is $\ge 24\text{h}$ old while honoring `auto_update_default` (`#2095`), supports `ManualProvider` and OpenClaw multi-command plans (`#2108`, `#2107`), and removes redundant `os.Chmod` on every CLI run (`#2102`).
4. **Coinbase AgentKit `uploadMedia` `cwd` Confinement, x402 `beforePayment` Screening, `ActionRefVerify` Content-Addressed Binding & `Beaver Knight` Trust Score (`agentkit#1459`, `#1458`, `#1457`, `#1455`, `#1454`, `#1453`, `#1452`, `#1448`, `#1444`, `#1431`, `#1430`, `#1426`, `PR-11`)**:
   - Confines `uploadMedia` file paths to `cwd` via `realpath` resolution to block prompt-injected path traversal (`../../etc/passwd`, `#1454`), runs the x402 `beforePayment` recipient-screening hook before payment (`#1453`), verifies deterministic `aref_` content-addressed action references in `ActionRefVerify` (`#1457`), and checks `Beaver Knight` trust ratings (`#1452`) and read-only `TaskMarket` discovery (`#1458`).

### 8.4 MCP 6-Language SDKs (Go / Rust / Python / TypeScript / C# / Java) & Inspector (`PR-12..PR-16`)
1. **MCP Go `oauthex` DCR 1 MiB Cap, Supported Version Allowlist, `subscriptions/listen` Pruning & `SSEServerTransport` Drain (`go-sdk#1219`, `#1217`, `#1216`, `#1206`, `#1203`, `#1199`, `#1196`, `#1195`, `#1192`, `#1190`, `#1186`, `#1166`, `PR-12`)**:
   - Bounds OAuth Dynamic Client Registration (`oauthex` DCR) response reads to `1 MiB` (`#1219`), restricts server-supported MCP protocol versions (`#1216`), prunes completed `subscriptions/listen` request IDs and fixes `serverSession.Close()` drain deadlocks (`#1203`), and drains buffered SSE messages before closing `SSEServerTransport` while wrapping underlying write errors (`io.EOF`) inside server-closing errors (`#1199`, `#1192`).
2. **MCP Rust Retired-Session Late-Reply Rejection, Strict `Origin` `403`, Sessionless `server/discover` Fallback & 40-Hex Action SHA Pin (`rust-sdk#1221`, `#1219`, `#1217`, `#1216`, `#1211`, `#1206`, `#1196`, `#1195`, `#1192`, `#1186`, `PR-13`)**:
   - Rejects late replies from retired Streamable HTTP sessions (`#1217`), returns `403 Forbidden` on missing/malformed/non-UTF-8 `Origin` headers under strict `Origin` validation (`#1216`, `#1196`), falls back after sessionless `server/discover` rejection in `ClientLifecycleMode::Auto` (`#1221`), allows concurrent Streamable HTTP POST requests (`#1219`), and pins CI GitHub Actions to 40-hex commit SHAs (`#1211`).
3. **MCP Python Case-Insensitive `Host`/`Origin`, Custom `request_id` Counter Advance, `NaN`/`Infinity` $\to$ `null` & Idle Session Eviction (`python-sdk#3458`, `#3453`, `#3450`, `#3445`, `#3444`, `#3438`, `#3433`, `#3429`, `#3428`, `#3420`, `PR-14`)**:
   - Matches `Host` and `Origin` headers case-insensitively per RFC 9110 (`#3450`), advances the internal request-ID counter past caller-supplied `request_id` values (`#3438`), serializes non-finite floats (`NaN`, `Infinity`) as `null` in JSON text content blocks (`#3433`), expires idle Streamable HTTP sessions while capping concurrent sessions (`#3445`), and stops GET stream retries on HTTP `405` (`#3428`).
4. **MCP TypeScript `InMemoryTransport` Queue Order & Close Drain, Cached `listTools` Fallback, Same-Chunk Progress-Before-Result & `ReadBuffer` Linear Growth (`typescript-sdk#2984`, `#2983`, `#2982`, `#2981`, `#2980`, `#2979`, `#2978`, `#2976`, `#2974`, `#2968`, `#2967`, `#2965`, `PR-15`)**:
   - Preserves queued `InMemoryTransport` message FIFO order and discards queued messages on `close()` (`#2982`, `#2981`), retains cached tool metadata when `listTools()` refresh fails (`#2984`), delivers progress notifications arriving in the same chunk before the final response (`#2983`), and avoids quadratic $O(N^2)$ buffer copying in `ReadBuffer` (`#2976`).
5. **MCP C# Integer `ProgressToken` & Probe Timeout Survival, Java `200` Invalid JSON Error & `U+2028` Non-Truncation, and Inspector `400` Era Fallback & Confidential Client Secret Carry (`csharp-sdk#1834..#1826`, `java-sdk#1161..#1138` & `inspector#2684..#2664`, `PR-16`)**:
   - Preserves integer `ProgressToken` JSON numbers and keeps interactive OAuth alive when `DiscoverProbeTimeout` cancels the probe (`#1834`, `#1832`); rejects invalid `jsonrpc` response versions, surfaces invalid JSON in `200` Streamable HTTP responses as transport errors, and does not truncate SSE fields on `U+2028`/`U+2029`/`U+0085` (`#1161`, `#1150`, `#1138`); and relays upstream `400` status in Inspector so `auto` era falls back to 2025 Streamable HTTP while carrying stripped-namespace confidential DCR client secrets before purging (`#2684`, `#2678`, `#2671`).

## 9. Stages 278–279 Deep-Dive Audit (`972/972` Unit Tests Passing · 238 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs!): Coinbase x402 `eip3009` EIP-712 Domain Mapping & `SAR` Settlement Attestation Receipt, AgentKit Python ERC-20 Integer Receipt Status & `cbBTC` 8-Decimal Resolution & SPL Base-Unit Rounding, UCP RFC 9421 §3.3.3 Raw 64-Byte `r||s` ECDSA & Python SDK Conditional Schemas & Conformance HTTPS Discovery Allowlist, ACP Mutating Tool `Idempotency-Key` & `delegate_payment` Signature MUST, A2A RFC 6598 CGNAT (`100.64.0.0/10`) SSRF Guard & Go Trailing JSON Rejection, Stripe 7-SDK Blank Webhook Secret Guard & CLI Positional ID Escaping, and MCP 9-SDK & Inspector/Registry Deterministic Guards

### 9.1 Coinbase `x402` & `agentkit` (`PR-01..PR-02`)
1. **Coinbase `x402` `exact/evm` `eip3009` EIP-712 Domain Mapping, `SAR` Settlement Attestation Receipt, `no-wallet` Voucher & `solidarity` Split Cap (`coinbase/x402#420`, `#419`, `#414`, `#413`, `#346`, `#334`, `#324`, `#323`, `#322`, `#317`, `#50`, `#48`, `#46`, `#44`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Enforces `exact/evm` `eip3009` EIP-712 `domain.name`/`domain.version` mapping from `PaymentRequirements.extra` (`#420`), verifies Settlement Attestation Receipt (`SAR`) witness signatures, non-zero `txHash`, and `paymentHash` binding (`#414`), validates `no-wallet` voucher recipient/expiry (`#419`), and caps `solidarity` split basis points at $\le 10000\text{ bps}$ (`#413`).
2. **Coinbase AgentKit Python ERC-20 Integer Receipt `status == 1`, CDP Smart Wallet Bundle Revert Check, Moonwell `cbBTC` 8 Decimals, SPL Base-Unit Rounding & Pre-Payment Guards (`coinbase/agentkit#1598`, `#1597`, `#1593`, `#1591`, `#1590`, `#1589`, `#1581`, `#1579`, `#1575`, `#1572`, `#1567`, `#1564`, `#1563`, `#1559`, `#1557`, `#1550`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Checks integer receipt `receipt["status"] == 1` in Python ERC-20 actions (`#1597`), rejects reverted subcalls in CDP Smart Wallet bundles (`#1550`), resolves Moonwell `cbBTC` with 8 decimals instead of 18 (`#1557`), enforces integer base-unit rounding in SPL/Jupiter transfers (`#1559`), and gates payments via `prePaymentCheck`, `CanaryGuard`, and `token_verdict` (`#1598`, `#1593`, `#1591`).

### 9.2 UCP Core Spec, `python-sdk`, `conformance`, `samples` & ACP Checkout (`PR-03..PR-06`)
1. **UCP RFC 9421 §3.3.3 Raw 64-Byte `r||s` ECDSA P-256 Signature, Return Eligibility/Deadlines/Fees, Standardized Fulfillment Events & Root-Array Schema Ban (`ucp#984`, `#982`, `#981`, `#980`, `#979`, `#977`, `#976`, `#975`, `#974`, `#966`, `#964`, `#963`, `#948`, `#946`, `#945`, `#940`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Requires RFC 9421 §3.3.3 ECDSA P-256 signatures to use raw 64-byte fixed-width `r||s` concatenation (rejecting `0x30`-prefixed ASN.1 DER encodings, `#979`), enforces explicit `return_policy` eligibility, window days, and fee rules (`#966`), restricts fulfillment events to standardized types (`#975`), and forbids root-array JSON schemas (`#948`).
2. **UCP `python-sdk` `dependentRequired`, EC JWK Conditional Rules, `maxProperties`, `additionalProperties: false` & Removing Regex Patterns from Generated `datetime` Fields (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/python-sdk#101`, `#100`, `#99`, `#96`, `#94`, `#91`, `#89`, `#87`, `#83`, `#76`, `#74`, `#70`, `#69`, `#68`, `#66`, `#64`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Enforces `dependentRequired` constraints (`#101`), requires `crv`, `x`, and `y` on `kty == "EC"` JWKs (`#100`), checks `maxProperties` and closed-model `additionalProperties: false` (`#99`, `#94`), and strips conflicting regex `pattern` constraints from generated `datetime` fields (`#89`).
3. **UCP `conformance` & `samples` Strict HTTPS Discovery Allowlist, Payload-Based Webhook Identity, Canonical `signing_keys[]` Path & Single Payment Instrument Completion (`conformance#113`, `#111`, `#110`, `#109`, `#98`, `#97`, `#88`, `#85`, `#82`, `#76` & `samples#253`, `#252`, `#249`, `#248`, `#247`, `#246`, `#228`, `#226`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Restricts merchant discovery URLs to HTTPS allowlisted origins (`conformance#113`), derives webhook event identity from signed payload `order.id` rather than unsigned `X-Event-Type` headers (`#111`), requires canonical top-level `signing_keys[]` (`#110`), and enforces a single payment instrument on checkout completion with catalog product existence checks (`#85`, `samples#248`).
4. **ACP Mutating Tool `Idempotency-Key`, `delegate_payment`/`delegate_authentication` `Signature`+`Timestamp` MUST, Host Spend Confirmation & Schema ↔ OpenAPI Drift Gate (`agentic-commerce-protocol#314`, `#310`, `#305`, `#296`, `#289`, `#288`, `#287`, `#285`, `#284`, `#281`, `#275`, `#266`, `#262`, `#260`, `#256`, `#254`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Requires non-empty `Idempotency-Key` headers on mutating MCP checkout tools (`#288`), upgrades `Signature` and `Timestamp` to `MUST` on `delegate_payment` and `delegate_authentication` (`#287`, `#285`), enforces host-side user spend authorization (`#314`), and blocks Schema ↔ OpenAPI operation drift (`#310`).

### 9.3 Google A2A Spec & 6-Language SDKs / Inspector, and Stripe 7-Language SDKs / CLI / Agent-Toolkit (`PR-07..PR-10`)
1. **A2A Spec & `a2a-python` RFC 6598 CGNAT (`100.64.0.0/10`) SSRF Block, Pre-JCS Field Preservation, Terminal `status.timestamp` Auto-Stamping & Parked Interrupted Task Timeout (`A2A#2378..#2319` & `a2a-python#1364..#1326`, `PR-07`)**:

   - Rejects trailing JSON tokens after the first value in `a2a-go` (`#453`) and lone-surrogate Unicode in AgentCard signing inputs (`#450`), blocks payload tenant overrides of HTTP-authenticated tenants in `a2a-dotnet` (`#539`), and requires a non-empty push config ID on `DeleteTaskPushNotificationConfig` in `a2a-js` (`#774`).

   - URL-escapes positional resource IDs in `stripe-cli` (`#2125`), reaps exited peer plugins (`#2150`), and blocks silent major-version auto-upgrades (`#2141`); returns immediate JSON-RPC forwarding errors (`agent-toolkit#542`) and aborts abandoned LLM token-meter streams (`#509`).
4. **Stripe 7-Language SDKs (Python / Node / Go / .NET / Java / Ruby / PHP) Blank Webhook Secret Guard, PHP Retry `Idempotency-Key` Preservation & .NET Ordinal Path Comparison (`PR-10`)**:

### 9.4 Model Context Protocol 9-Language SDKs, Inspector & Registry (`PR-11..PR-16`)

## 10. Stages 280–281 Milestone Deep-Dive Audit (280-Stage Milestone & `976/976` Unit Tests Passing · 293 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 40 Upstream Repositories!): Coinbase x402 Streamed Request Cloning & AgentKit CDP `idempotencyKey`, AP2 dSD-JWT Terminal Binding & Visa TAP `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` Post-Quantum Hybrid mTLS, UCP `304 Not Modified` Profile Cache & `requires_escalation` `continue_url`, ACP OpenAPI Inline `$ref` Validation & Integer Minor-Unit Refunds, A2A Push-Failure Isolation & Multi-SDK Streaming/Tenant Routing, Stripe `tulip` Admission Gate & 7-SDK Account Scoping, Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Knowledge-Base Sandbox & Bijective 1-to-1 Action Matching, and MCP 11-Language SDK / Conformance / Inspector / Registry Guards

### 10.1 Coinbase `x402`, `agentkit`, Google `AP2` & Visa `trusted-agent-protocol` (`PR-01..PR-02`)
1. **Coinbase `x402-fetch` Streamed Request Body Cloning, `accepts[]` Fault Tolerance & AgentKit CDP `idempotencyKey` / Auto-Pay Ceiling (`coinbase/x402#312`, `#306`, `#297`, `#283`, `#277`, `#257`, `#412`, `#408`, `#391`, `#390` & `coinbase/agentkit#1530`, `#1507`, `#1468`, `#1469`, `#1545`, `#1552`, `#1554`, `#1491`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Clones `Request` before the initial `fetch` so `ReadableStream` bodies are not exhausted on `402 Payment Required` retries (`x402#306`), skips malformed `accepts[]` entries instead of crashing (`#297`), fails loudly when `toClientEvmSigner` lacks an address (`#277`), forwards `idempotencyKey` on CDP value-moving actions (`agentkit#1530`), compares EVM addresses case-insensitively (`#1507`), and binds x402 quote + settlement identity + `auto-pay` limits (`#1468`).
2. **Google AP2 dSD-JWT Terminal `aud`/`nonce`, ISO 4217 0-Decimal Budget Caps, Atomic Mandate Consumption & Visa TAP `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` Hybrid PQC mTLS (`AP2#384`, `#383`, `#378`, `#377`, `#368`, `#365`, `#363`, `#359`, `#351`, `#347`, `#342`, `#335`, `#330`, `#326`, `#324`, `#318` & `visa/trusted-agent-protocol#18`, `#9`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Checks `has_reference_in_store_cb` before invoking in `verify_receipt` (`#384`), dumps `cnf.jwk` in JSON mode so keys with `"use"` verify (`#383`), enforces ISO 8601 `payment.execution_date` bounds and 0-decimal (JPY) minor-unit conversion (`#378`, `#377`, `#368`), requires terminal `aud`/`nonce`, closed checkout binding, and atomic single consumption of accepted mandates (`#347`, `#342`, `#330`), and verifies Visa TAP DID `key_id` resolution, `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` hybrid signatures, and mTLS client cert authorization (`#18`, `#9`).

### 10.2 UCP Core Spec, `python-sdk`, `conformance`, `samples` & ACP Checkout (`PR-03..PR-06`)
1. **UCP `304 Not Modified` Profile Cache Hit, `requires_escalation` `continue_url`, Omitting `cart_id` on `complete_checkout` & Empty `payment` Object for Deferred Terms (`ucp#939`, `#938`, `#934`, `#933`, `#932`, `#930`, `#928`, `#918`, `#917`, `#916`, `#915`, `#914`, `#913`, `#912`, `#911`, `#908`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Treats HTTP `304 Not Modified` as a valid cache hit rather than a redirect error during profile fetching (`#933`, `#932`), requires `continue_url` when checkout status is `requires_escalation` (`#912`), omits `cart_id` on `complete_checkout` and permits `payment: {}` for deferred payment terms (`#911`, `#908`), and requires CPs to verify AP2 payment mandates before issuing PSP tokens (`#916`).
2. **UCP `python-sdk` Class-Scoped `uniqueItems`, Array `contains`/`minContains`/`maxContains`, `minProperties` & `UcpMetadata` Catalog Union (`python-sdk#98`..`#52`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Scopes `uniqueItems` validators to declaring classes and generated array fields (`#61`, `#59`), enforces array `contains` bounds and model `minProperties` (`#57`, `#55`), and derives `UcpMetadata` union from `$defs` so `catalog` is included (`#58`).
3. **UCP `conformance` & `samples` Discovered Endpoint Routing, Line-Item Quantity ($>0$), Sparse Order Serialization & Missing-Order UCP Envelope (`conformance#93`..`#73` & `samples#243`..`#214`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Routes `invalid_input` order probes through discovered endpoints (`#75`), accepts `200`/`201` on checkout creation (`#78`), validates line-item quantity $>0$ on create/update (`samples#243`), stops `null`-padding unset optional order fields (`#224`), and returns standard UCP error envelopes on missing orders (`#214`).
4. **ACP OpenAPI Inline `$ref` Example Validation, Unwrapped `delegate_payment` Error Examples & Integer Minor-Unit Refund Amounts (`agentic-commerce-protocol#309`..`#219`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Validates OpenAPI inline examples against `$ref` schemas (`#252`), unwraps redundant error envelopes in `delegate_payment` examples (`#243`), enforces positive integer minor-unit refund amounts (`#249`, `#219`), and adds `session_context` telemetry (`#238`).

### 10.3 Google A2A 5-Language SDKs, Stripe Suite / 7-Language SDKs & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 (`PR-07..PR-10`)
1. **A2A Spec & `a2a-python` Push Notification Failure Isolation, Releasing `ActiveTask` After Direct `Message`, Suppressing Malformed JSON-RPC Tracebacks & Non-`Status` REST Errors (`A2A#2384..#2285` & `a2a-python#1348..#1287`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Isolates push notification failures from aborting task lifecycle transitions (`#1329`), releases `ActiveTask` after direct `Message` responses (`#1297`), keeps follow-up messages when executors re-emit the current task (`#1298`), suppresses stderr tracebacks on malformed JSON-RPC bodies (`#1301`), and handles non-`google.rpc.Status` REST error responses (`#1300`).
2. **A2A Multi-Language SDKs (JS / Go / Java / .NET / Inspector) JSON-RPC Error HTTP 200, Underscore Task Page Tokens, Terminal Enqueue Recovery & Multi-Tenant Router (`a2a-js#764..#744`, `a2a-go#467..#437`, `a2a-java#1199..#1184`, `a2a-dotnet#572..#568` & `a2a-inspector#213`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Returns HTTP `200` for JSON-RPC errors under `A2A-Version`, validates media types against `defaultInputModes`, and refuses unapplied DB migration downgrades in JS (`#755`, `#747`, `#745`); surfaces `application/json` streaming errors and preserves underscores in task ID page tokens in Go (`#467`, `#459`); recovers failed terminal enqueue events and resolves multi-tenant `AgentCard` via router in Java (`#1185`, `#1184`); and requires opt-in for loopback push destinations in .NET (`#568`).
3. **Stripe Agent-Toolkit `tulip` Admission Gate, Dot-Form Model ID Lookup, CLI Known-Plugin Auto-Install Guard & 7-SDK Event Account Scoping (`agent-toolkit#560..#464`, `stripe-cli#2149..#2082` & 7 SDKs, `PR-09`)**:
   - Enforces the `tulip` admission gate in `run_tool()` across all 4 framework adapters (`#482`), supports both dash-form and dot-form model IDs in `getDefaultMaxTokens` (`#464`), restricts CLI backend auto-install to known plugins (`#2084`), scopes event handler clients to the event account across Python/Java/PHP (`#1911`, `#2294`, `#2151`), and safely ignores malformed webhook signature items in PHP (`#2170`).
4. **Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Knowledge-Base Sandbox Boundary, Minimal Shell Env, Bijective 1-to-1 Gold Action Matching & Same-Item Exchange Rejection (`tau2-bench#677..#632`, `PR-10`)**:
   - Denies sandbox reads outside the knowledge base (`#655`), runs sandboxed shells with a minimal environment (`#653`), requires bijective 1-to-1 matching between gold actions and tool calls (`#665`), rejects same-item exchanges in retail (`#667`), and treats empty `mmsc_url` as unconfigured in telecom (`#666`).

### 10.4 Model Context Protocol 11-Language SDKs, Conformance, Inspector & Registry (`PR-11..PR-16`)
1. **MCP TypeScript & Python SDKs URI Fragment Exclusion (`#`), Clearing Stale Stdio Frames, RFC 9449 DPoP Proof Binding & Completion Pagination (`typescript-sdk#3087..#2993` & `python-sdk#3746..#3660`, `PR-11`)**:
   - Excludes `#` fragment delimiters from URI template matches (`#3010`) and clears stale stdio frames before restart in TS (`#2993`); attaches RFC 9449 DPoP proofs on token requests (`#3672`), base64-encodes `bytes` tool output (`#3667`), prevents SSE server crashes on invalid `Host`/`Origin` (`#3663`), and paginates completions $>100$ items in Python (`#3660`).
2. **MCP Go & Rust SDKs Pre-`initialize` Invalid Request (`-32600`), Cancellation Binding Before ID Reuse, Redacted Metadata Credentials & `2026-07-28` Stateless Guard (`go-sdk#1420..#1378` & `rust-sdk#1370..#1306`, `PR-12`)**:
   - Rejects pre-`initialize` requests with `-32600 Invalid Request` (`#1393`), binds peer cancellation before request ID reuse (`#1390`), and redacts credentials in protected resource metadata in Go (`#1396`); rejects server-to-client requests on `2026-07-28` sessions (`#1326`), validates `Host`/`Origin` literals (`#1343`, `#1319`), and rejects multi-line auth secrets in Rust (`#1306`).
3. **MCP C#, Java & Kotlin SDKs Discovered Issuer as `ID-JAG` Audience, Bare-`\r` Framing Guard & RFC 3986 SSE Endpoint Resolution (`csharp-sdk#1943..#1890`, `java-sdk#1271..#1258` & `kotlin-sdk#1130..#1100`, `PR-13`)**:
   - Uses discovered AS issuer as `ID-JAG` token-exchange audience (`#1890`), does not misreport cancellation as missing OAuth metadata (`#1891`), and splits comma-separated URI template variables in C# (`#1900`); guards against bare `\r` premature message termination in Java (`#1264`); and resolves SSE endpoints per RFC 3986 while keeping `StdioServerTransport` alive on handler cancellation in Kotlin (`#1100`, `#1101`).
4. **MCP Swift SDK PKCE Modulo-Bias Byte Rejection, Retained Refresh Tokens, Header-Only Data URL Base64 Detection & Stateless In-Flight ID Collision Guard (`swift-sdk#313..#267`, `PR-14`)**:
   - Rejects modulo-biased random bytes during PKCE verifier generation (`#303`), validates OAuth issuers and registration metadata (`#293`), retains omitted refresh tokens (`#313`), detects base64 strictly from data URL headers (`#309`, `#295`, `#278`), and rejects colliding in-flight JSON-RPC IDs on stateless HTTP (`#267`).
5. **MCP Ruby & PHP SDKs `Authorization` Error Redaction, Non-UTF-8 Key Parse Error (`-32700`), Parallel Elicitation Isolation & HTTP `503` on Unreachable Session Store (`ruby-sdk#629..#583` & `php-sdk#606..#570`, `PR-15`)**:
   - Redacts `Authorization` headers from retained HTTP errors (`#591`), rejects non-`"2.0"` JSON-RPC messages (`#590`), and treats non-UTF-8 JSON keys as `-32700` parse errors in Ruby (`#624`); validates `Host` and `Origin` independently (`#589`), isolates parallel elicitation answers (`#581`), avoids bundling queued notifications into request JSON responses (`#602`), and returns HTTP `503` (not `404`) when the session store is unreachable in PHP (`#570`).
6. **MCP Conformance, Inspector, Registry & Spec SEP-2243 (`-32020`), SEP-1932 DPoP Binding & Downgrade Guard, Stripping `npm_config_allow_scripts` & Verifiable DB Backups (`conformance#560..#508`, `inspector#2747..#2713`, `registry#1735..#1726` & `modelcontextprotocol#3415..#3405`, `PR-16`)**:
   - Requires `-32020` on SEP-2243 standard-header mismatches (`#555`), `-32002` on resource-not-found (`#554`), prevents reject-all servers from passing scored scenarios (`#537`), and enforces SEP-1932 `dpop_jkt` binding and Bearer-downgrade rejection in Conformance (`#528`..`#523`); strips inherited `npm_config_allow_scripts` to block unreviewed install scripts (`#2713`, `#2714`) and requires `ttlMs` and `cacheScope` on directory reads in Inspector (`#2747`); and produces verifiable DB backups in Registry (`#1726`).

## 11. Stages 282–283 Deep-Dive Audit (`980/980` Unit Tests Passing · 356 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 49 Upstream Repositories!): A2A-x402 CSPRNG Nonce & Post-Settlement Completion, `x402-rs` On-Chain Settle Retry & RPC Redaction, MPP Atomic Stream Reservations & Tempo Voucher Migration, Visa MCP Lexicographic X-Pay Token & TAP `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65`, AP2 Dual-Language Corpus & RFC 9901 SD-JWT / WBA, UCP WBA Interop & Constrained Schema Twin Isolation, ACP Delegate Auth & Discount Extension, A2A Remote Attestation & 6-SDK Guards, Stripe WIF Trusted Publishing & AgentKit `RelayShield` / `M2M Sentinel`, and MCP `SEP-3246` 11-SDK Guards

### 11.1 `a2a-x402`, `x402-rs`, `CAIPs`, `wevm/mppx` & `tempoxyz/tempo` (`PR-01..PR-03`)
1. **Google `a2a-x402` Random EIP-3009 Nonce, Deferred Paid Response Completion Until Settlement Finality & StepSecurity Pinning (`a2a-x402#190`, `#186`, `#172` & `coinbase/x402#316`..`#305`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Enforces CSPRNG random nonces for EIP-3009 authorizations (`a2a-x402#190`), defers paid A2A response completion until settlement finishes (`#186`), and pins StepSecurity CI workflows (`coinbase/x402#313`).
2. **`x402-rs` On-Chain Settle Retry Reconciliation, `settlement_pending` Hash Preservation on Receipt Timeout, RPC URL Redaction & CAIP-397 CWP (`x402-rs#142`, `#140`, `#138`, `#118` & `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#411`, `#404`, `#397`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Answers settle retries with missing store entries by reconciling broadcast transactions on-chain (`#142`, `#140`), preserves the transaction hash and returns `settlement_pending` when receipt fetching fails (`#138`), redacts provider RPC URLs in on-chain failure details (`#118`), and validates CAIP-397 CLI Wallet Protocol and CAIP-411 Label ID specifications.
3. **MPP `mppx` Atomic Stream Reservations, Detected-Close Settlement & Tempo Retired Root-Key Rejection and Voucher Migration (`wevm/mppx#992`, `#991`, `#990`, `#989` & `tempoxyz/tempo#8558`, `#8557`, `#8554`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Shares MPP stream reservations atomically (`#990`), settles accepted funds on detected session close (`#991`), suppresses duplicate payment events for existing authorizations (`#989`), rejects retired root keys in Tempo Zone RPC auth tokens (`#8558`), and keeps funded channel vouchers redeemable after precompile migration (`#8557`).

### 11.2 Visa `mcp` / `trusted-agent-protocol`, AP2, SD-JWT, Web Bot Auth, UCP & ACP (`PR-04..PR-07`)
1. **Visa MCP Lexicographic `X-Pay-Token` Query Sorting, JWKS/PAN Log Redaction & Visa TAP DID `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` Hybrid Signature (`visa/mcp#21`..`#9` & `visa/trusted-agent-protocol#19`..`#1`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Sorts query parameters lexicographically for `X-Pay-Token` computation and extracts headers case-insensitively (`visa/mcp#21`), validates JWKS chains, scopes token deletion, and redacts card data logs (`#17`, `#16`), adds AffixIO `ACTION` governance for VIC agent spend (`#18`), and verifies DID `key_id` resolution with `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` hybrid post-quantum signatures in Visa TAP (`#17`).
2. **AP2 Dual-Language Mandate Conformance Corpus, RFC 9901 SD-JWT Audit, Cloudflare Web Bot Auth Unique Labels & HTTPS Directory, and MCP `ext-auth` DPoP `dpop_jkt` Binding (`AP2#382`..`#352`, `sd-jwt-js#394`..`#386`, `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#170`..`#161` & `ext-auth#41`..`#35`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Verifies AP2 mandates against both reference and second-language conformance verifiers (`#370`, `#369`), resolves RFC 9901 audit findings and `vct` claim tests in `sd-jwt-js` (`#389`, `#387`), rejects duplicate WBA signature labels and requires HTTPS `Signature-Agent` directory members (`#169`, `#163`), and binds CIMD `client_id` and authorization code `dpop_jkt` in `ext-auth` (`#41`, `#35`).
3. **UCP Web Bot Auth Interop Backport, Checkout Payment Finality & JS/Python SDK Constrained Schema Twin Isolation (`ucp#829`..`#814`, `python-sdk#95`..`#75`, `js-sdk#89`..`#82` & `samples#261`..`#256`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Backports WBA interop and shopping permalink capabilities (`#829`, `#828`) and defines checkout payment finality and order reporting (`#816`); preserves top-level `required` on `allOf` schemas and prevents constrained schemas from leaking onto unconstrained twins in `js-sdk` (`#89`, `#83`); and resolves local entity references before inlining in `python-sdk` (`#79`).
4. **ACP Delegate Authentication Governance, Discount Extension Reference Validation & Meta TSC/CLA Signatory Guard (`agentic-commerce-protocol#269`..`#203`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Validates delegate authentication and governance docs, discount extension links, `2026-01-16` changelog listings (`#269`, `#267`, `#251`), and TSC/CLA governance bindings (`#263`, `#236`).

### 11.3 Google A2A 6-Language SDKs, Stripe Suite / 7-Language SDKs, AgentKit & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 (`PR-08..PR-12`)
1. **A2A Hardware-Rooted Remote Attestation, ADR-002 Coherent Task Timeline & `a2a-python`/`a2a-js` Stale `ActiveTask` Snapshot Guard (`A2A#2092`..`#2081`, `a2a-python#1233`..`#1222` & `a2a-js#796`..`#790`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Specifies hardware-rooted remote attestation (`#2088`) and ADR-002 coherent task history (`#2084`); compares in-memory task timestamps numerically, drops stale `ActiveTask` snapshots when reusing idle tasks, surfaces producer errors after failed tasks, and validates `A2A-Version` minor version in `a2a-python` (`#1233`, `#1229`, `#1227`, `#1225`); and passes history read hints to task stores in `a2a-js` (`#795`).
2. **A2A Go / Java / .NET / Inspector `HEAD` Subscription Rejection, In-Place Artifact Recovery & Optional Push Config IDs (`a2a-go#487`..`#479`, `a2a-java#1103`..`#1091`, `a2a-dotnet#586`..`#582` & `a2a-inspector#218`..`#211`, `PR-09`)**:
   - Rejects `HEAD` before creating task subscriptions (`#481`), recovers full artifacts on in-place mutation (`#485`), supports `\r` SSE line endings (`#487`), and normalizes service param keys in `a2a-go` (`#479`); allows omitted task push config IDs and optional `FilePart` names in `a2a-java` (`#1092`, `#1091`); and hardens `a2a-inspector` with the Go backend and DOMPurify `3.4.17` (`#218`, `#215`).
3. **Stripe Agent-Toolkit Threadless Sync Tool Execution, Provider-Neutral AI Plugin Hooks & CLI Sandbox Change Stamping (`stripe/ai#579`..`#552`, `agent-toolkit#581`..`#515` & `stripe-cli#2148`..`#2138`, `PR-10`)**:
   - Runs sync LangChain/CrewAI tool calls safely when the worker thread has no event loop and warns on deprecated `'actions'` config keys (`#581`, `#559`); refactors plugin hooks to be provider-neutral and opts out of CLI telemetry during Claude startup checks (`#569`, `#567`); and stamps sandbox changes while explaining pinned-version plugin install failures with a second lookup (`#2146`, `#2143`).
4. **Stripe 7-Language SDK Workload Identities Package (WIF) Trusted Publishing, Node `fetch` Dispose & API v2 Search (`stripe-python#1955`..`#1949`, `stripe-node#2910`..`#2903`, `stripe-go#2478`..`#2473`, `stripe-java#2320`..`#2318`, `stripe-dotnet#3483`..`#3480`, `stripe-ruby#2006`..`#2003` & `stripe-php#2179`..`#2177`, `PR-11`)**:
   - Enforces release-environment protection and trusted publishing for the Workload Identities Package (WIF) (`#2904`, `#2002`, `#2000`, `#2318`), exports `V2List`/`V2ListPromise` and fixes `fetch` dispose leaks in `stripe-node` (`#2901`, `#2896`), adds API v2 Search across .NET/Java/PHP (`#3476`, `#2312`, `#2171`), and updates master TLS certificates in `stripe-python` (`#1955`).
5. **Coinbase AgentKit `RelayShield` / `M2M Sentinel` / `Agent Guild` / Base USDC `Offramp` & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Submission Verification (`agentkit#1449`..`#1432` & `tau2-bench#682`..`#651`, `PR-12`)**:
   - Adds `RelayShield` (`#1449`), `M2M Sentinel` for Base bytecode/proxy observations (`#1447`), `Agent Guild` trust and payment safety (`#1446`), and `OfframpActionProvider` for Base USDC cash-out (`#1442`); and dates/verifies `Ava Zero` and `phonic-1` audio-native submissions in $\tau$-bench 2.0 (`#676`, `#675`, `#670`).

### 11.4 Model Context Protocol 11-Language SDKs, Inspector & Registry (`PR-13..PR-16`)
1. **MCP `SEP-3246` Provenance-Carrying Authority Confinement, Python Validation Input Redaction & TypeScript `expectedResource` / Cached Ajv (`modelcontextprotocol#3256`..`#3244`, `python-sdk#3595`..`#3582` & `typescript-sdk#2954`..`#2947`, `PR-13`)**:
   - Enforces `SEP-3246` provenance-carrying authority confinement for delegated tool calls (`#3246`); hides raw input values from tool validation error messages, contains streamable HTTP POST transport errors, and lets `stdio_server` exit while the peer holds stdin open in `python-sdk` (`#3595`, `#3584`, `#3582`); and adds `expectedResource` to `requireBearerAuth` while reusing compiled Ajv validators in `typescript-sdk` (`#2952`, `#2950`).
2. **MCP Go / Rust / Java SDK Stream Subscription Cleanup, Bounded Streamable HTTP Buffers & Unsupported `listRoots` Rejection (`go-sdk#1290`..`#1279`, `rust-sdk#1252`..`#1243` & `java-sdk#1149`..`#1139`, `PR-14`)**:
   - Clears resource subscriptions when listen streams end and preserves explicit `null` structured content in `go-sdk` (`#1285`, `#1283`); bounds buffered Streamable HTTP responses and keeps discover lifecycle bootstrap-neutral in `rust-sdk` (`#1248`, `#1246`); and rejects `listRoots` when unsupported while preserving `U+2028`/`U+2029`/`U+0085` in SSE field values in `java-sdk` (`#1144`, `#1142`).
3. **MCP C# / Kotlin / Swift SDK Degenerate Base64 Header Wrapper Fix, Hono/Zod Interop & Cached Swift Actor Codecs (`csharp-sdk#1809`..`#1801`, `kotlin-sdk#1133`..`#1123` & `swift-sdk#308`..`#289`, `PR-15`)**:
   - Prevents `McpHeaderEncoder.DecodeValue` from throwing on degenerate base64 wrappers in `csharp-sdk` (`#1805`), upgrades Hono `4.13.13` / Zod `4.6.7` integration parity in `kotlin-sdk` (`#1133`, `#1126`), and removes `[weak self]` in actor closures while caching `Server` JSON encoders/decoders in `swift-sdk` (`#304`, `#299`).
4. **MCP PHP / Ruby / Inspector / Registry Event-Loop Fibers, Byte-Level Stdio Frame Stripping, Chained MRTR `requestState` & Orphan Secret Purge (`php-sdk#616`..`#600`, `ruby-sdk#631`..`#620`, `inspector#2610`..`#2599` & `registry#1732`..`#1727`, `PR-16`)**:
   - Drives request handler fibers via an event loop and sends HTTP requests outside the request fiber in `php-sdk` (`#616`, `#599`); strips stdio frames as bytes and omits `id` when refusing unreadable request IDs in `ruby-sdk` (`#625`, `#622`); chains MRTR rounds when the server rotates `requestState` and purges orphaned secret-store entries in `inspector` (`#2610`, `#2600`); and removes retired K8up schedules to prevent operator crashes while promoting `1.8.4` in `registry` (`#1732`, `#1727`).
5. **Stages 282–283 Engineering Assets & Verification**:
