## 12. Stages 284–285 Deep-Dive Audit (`984/984` Unit Tests Passing · 404 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 50 Upstream Repositories · 1,846 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!): `a2a-x402` Neutral `InterlineExecutor` & Payment Timeout Bounds, `x402-rs` ERC-8021 Calldata Tag & CAIP-27 `chainId`, MPP Atomic Settlement Claim & Tempo Reth Persistence Ack, Visa MCP VIC Validation & AP2 Every-KB-Hop `aud`/`nonce`, SD-JWT `_sd_alg` Registry & WBA Duplicate Component Rejection, UCP Lockfile Registry Block & Measure Integer Contract, ACP Swagger Semantic Validation & Product Feeds, A2A GET Subscribe & Multitenancy, Stripe Hermes Hardening & 7-SDK v2 Search, AgentKit SSH Confinement & Uniswap Zero-Quote Fail-Closed, and MCP `SEP-3221` 11-SDK Guards

### 12.1 `a2a-x402`, `x402-rs`, `CAIPs`, `wevm/mppx` & `tempoxyz/tempo` (`PR-01..PR-03`)
1. **Google `a2a-x402` Non-Custodial `InterlineExecutor` Router, Payment Timeout Validation & Trust Receipt Verification (`a2a-x402#136..#124` & `coinbase/x402#304..#290`, `PR-01`)**:
   - Routes non-custodial x402 settlements via `InterlineExecutor` (`#134`), validates payment timeout bounds (`#130`), and verifies Solana x402 trust receipts (`#126`).
2. **`x402-rs` ERC-8021 Attribution Tag on EIP-3009 Calldata, Malformed Money Rejection, Permit2 EIP-2612 Metadata & CAIP-27 `chainId` (`x402-rs#100..#86` & `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#394..#384`, `PR-02`)**:
   - Appends an ERC-8021 attribution tag to EIP-3009 settlement calldata (`#99`), rejects malformed money amounts (`#97`), aligns `v2-eip155-upto` and EIP-2612 Permit2 metadata (`#93`, `#88`), renames CAIP-27 `"scope"` to `"chainId"` (`#389`), and defines CAIP-394 GCIP and CAIP-390 cross-chain asset metadata (`#394`, `#390`).
3. **MPP `mppx` Atomic Scheduled Settlement Claim, Pre-Voucher Chain Refresh, Shared Replay Storage & Tempo Reth Persistence Ack (`wevm/mppx#982..#971` & `tempoxyz/tempo#8546..#8537`, `PR-03`)**:
   - Claims scheduled settlements atomically (`#982`), refreshes chain state before voucher acceptance (`#981`), requires shared replay storage for Stripe Tempo rails (`#977`), authorizes cooperative close credentials, and requires opt-in for sponsored access keys (`#976`, `#973`); Tempo acknowledges finalized blocks only after Reth persists them and checks lifecycle traces against a bounded TLA+ model (`#8544`, `#8541`).

### 12.2 Visa `mcp`, AP2, SD-JWT, Web Bot Auth, `ext-auth`, UCP & ACP (`PR-04..PR-07`)
1. **Visa MCP VIC Card Enrollment Validation, AP2 SD-JWT Delegation Chain Decoding, Every-KB-Hop `aud`/`nonce` & Fail-Closed x402 PSP (`visa/mcp#8..#1` & `AP2#350..#283`, `PR-04`)**:
   - Validates Visa Intelligent Commerce (VIC) card enrollment API responses, health checks, and structured logging (`visa/mcp#8`, `#6`, `#4`); fixes the AP2 SD-JWT decoder to handle delegation chains and verify expected `aud`/`nonce` on every Key-Binding hop (`AP2#350`, `#313`), preserves `PaymentInstrument` extension fields through signing (`#333`, `#329`), and fails closed in the x402 PSP when the `agent-provider` key is missing (`#310`).
2. **OWF `sd-jwt-js` `_sd_alg` Registry & Reserved Claim Rejection, Cloudflare WBA Duplicate Covered Component & Key-Alg Mismatch Rejection, and MCP `ext-auth` `id-jag-04` (`sd-jwt-js#385..#374`, `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#160..#149` & `ext-auth#33..#11`, `PR-05`)**:
   - Validates `_sd_alg` against the registry and rejects tampered/unreferenced disclosures and reserved claims on verify (`sd-jwt-js#383`, `#380`, `#378`); rejects duplicate covered components and `alg` parameters mismatching the resolved key in Web Bot Auth (`#158`, `#157`, `#154`); and aligns enterprise-managed authorization with `id-jag-04` while adding server identity and tool attestation (`ext-auth#33`, `#29`, `#17`).
3. **UCP Loyalty Extension Backport, Non-Public Lockfile Registry Block, JS SDK `Measure` Integer & `maxProperties`, and Body-Bound `Complete Checkout` & Scoped Cart Idempotency (`ucp#813..#802`, `conformance#108..#92`, `js-sdk#81..#69`, `python-sdk#54..#47` & `samples#251..#237`, `PR-06`)**:
   - Backports the loyalty extension and blocks non-public registries in lockfiles (`ucp#813`, `#806`), pins the declared `ucp-sdk` version in conformance (`#100`), enforces the shared `Measure` integer contract and `maxProperties` in generated JS schemas (`#71`, `#69`), binds `Complete Checkout` to the request body, scopes cart idempotency keys to operation and cart, and answers signature failures with the standard UCP error envelope (`samples#240`, `#239`, `#238`, `#237`).
4. **ACP `swagger-parser` Semantic OpenAPI Validation, Suggested Pricing, Fulfillment Details on Complete, Product Feeds & Cart Capability (`agentic-commerce-protocol#202..#175`, `PR-07`)**:
   - Enforces OpenAPI semantic validation with `swagger-parser` (`#202`) and specifies Suggested Pricing (`#201`), Fulfillment Details on Checkout Complete (`#200`), Product Feeds & Promotions APIs (`#192`, `#190`), and the Cart Capability (`#188`).

### 12.3 Google A2A 6-Language SDKs, Stripe Suite / 7-Language SDKs, AgentKit & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 (`PR-08..PR-12`)
1. **A2A `GET` `SubscribeToTask`, Python Push-Config Deep Copy & Non-Terminal `UNSPECIFIED`, and JS Typed SSE Errors (`A2A#2075..#2059`, `a2a-python#1220..#1202` & `a2a-js#789..#778`, `PR-08`)**:
   - Changes `SubscribeToTask` from `POST` to `GET` and rewrites JSON Schema `$ref`s to internal definitions (`#2075`, `#2068`); avoids out-of-range datetime ordering, deep-copies in-memory push configs, keeps `TASK_STATE_UNSPECIFIED` non-terminal, and flushes trailing SSE events in `a2a-python` (`#1220`, `#1218`, `#1217`, `#1216`); and throws typed A2A errors from JSON-RPC SSE error events in `a2a-js` (`#788`).
2. **A2A Go Nil `Typed.MarshalJSON`, Pre-First-Event Plain Streaming Errors, UTC Timestamps & Java Per-Tenant Routing (`a2a-go#474..#452`, `a2a-java#1089..#1079`, `a2a-dotnet#581..#576` & `a2a-inspector#210..#205`, `PR-09`)**:
   - Handles nil `Value` in `Typed.MarshalJSON`, maps non-A2A HTTP errors by status code, returns plain errors when streaming fails before the first event, and serializes task timestamps in UTC in `a2a-go` (`#474`, `#471`, `#463`, `#452`); and adds multitenancy support with per-tenant routing in `a2a-java` (`#1084`).
3. **Stripe Agent-Toolkit `SynapticChain` 256-Lane HTTP 402 Micro-Wallet, `NoContentGeneratedError`, AI Hermes Hardening & CLI Multi-OAuth Profiles (`agent-toolkit#506..#489`, `stripe/ai#550..#537` & `stripe-cli#2127..#2114`, `PR-10`)**:
   - Adds the `SynapticChain` 256-lane HTTP 402 micro-wallet tool and throws `NoContentGeneratedError` when a response has no choices (`#506`, `#500`); hardens Hermes plugin skills and warns on ignored config keys (`stripe/ai#532`, `#525`); and isolates multiple OAuth sessions via profiles in `stripe-cli` (`#2116`).
4. **Stripe 7-Language SDK API v2 Search, Node `V2List` CJS Exports & `fetch` Dispose, and Ruby WIF Trusted Publishing (`stripe-python#1945..#1938`, `stripe-node#2901..#2893`, `stripe-go#2472..#2463`, `stripe-java#2317..#2312`, `stripe-dotnet#3479..#3475`, `stripe-ruby#2002..#1998` & `stripe-php#2171..#2166`, `PR-11`)**:
   - Delivers API v2 Search across .NET/Java/PHP/Node/Ruby (`#3476`, `#2312`, `#2171`), hand-writes `V2List`/`V2ListPromise` CJS exports and disposes `fetch` responses in `stripe-node` (`#2901`, `#2896`), and configures RubyGems trusted publishing for the `wif` package in `stripe-ruby` (`#2002`, `#1998`).
5. **Coinbase AgentKit SSH Path Confinement, Non-HTTPS Image & Zero-Uniswap-Quote Fail-Closed, and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 6-Language Voice Benchmarks (`agentkit#1428..#1415` & `tau2-bench#649..#625`, `PR-12`)**:
   - Confines Python SSH `known_hosts_file` and upload/download paths to `cwd` or `~/.ssh` (`#1428`, `#1427`), rejects non-HTTPS remote images, and fails closed on missing or zero Uniswap quotes (`#1425`, `#1423`, `#1421`); and releases banking/entity voice benchmarks across six languages with call-level quality scoring in $\tau$-bench 2.0 (`#640`, `#637`).

### 12.4 Model Context Protocol 11-Language SDKs, Conformance, Inspector & Registry (`PR-13..PR-16`)
1. **MCP `SEP-3221` Secure Parameters, Python Stateless `GET` `405` & Preserved Auth Query Params, and TypeScript `ReadBuffer` Release (`modelcontextprotocol#3243..#3218`, `python-sdk#3581..#3567` & `typescript-sdk#2943..#2936`, `PR-13`)**:
   - Introduces `SEP-3221` Secure Parameters (`#3221`), returns HTTP `405` for listen-mode `GET` on stateless Streamable HTTP and preserves existing authorization endpoint query parameters in `python-sdk` (`#3574`, `#3567`), and releases consumed `ReadBuffer` storage in `typescript-sdk` (`#2943`).
2. **MCP Go Lazy Discovery Transport & Cancelled `2026-07-28` Stdio Silence, Rust Empty `cacheScope` & Enterprise `ID-JAG`, and Java `LAST_EVENT_ID` Stream Leak Fix (`go-sdk#1278..#1267`, `rust-sdk#1241..#1234` & `java-sdk#1137..#1126`, `PR-14`)**:
   - Lazily builds `defaultDiscoveryTransport`, retires only subscriptions registered by a given listen stream, and sends no response for cancelled requests on `2026-07-28` stdio sessions in `go-sdk` (`#1278`, `#1275`, `#1267`); treats empty `cacheScope` as omitted and adds enterprise `ID-JAG` exchanges in `rust-sdk` (`#1241`, `#1234`); and fixes stream leaks when resuming with `LAST_EVENT_ID` while sweeping hanging sessions in `java-sdk` (`#1130`, `#1127`).
3. **MCP C# Deterministic Streamable HTTP Status & Hybrid Session Mode, Kotlin `2.4.21` Parity, and Swift Backpressure Frame Isolation & Early Cancellation Fix (`csharp-sdk#1798..#1791`, `kotlin-sdk#1116..#1109` & `swift-sdk#280..#266`, `PR-15`)**:
   - Makes Streamable HTTP status mapping deterministic and adds hybrid stateful/stateless `HttpServerSessionMode` in `csharp-sdk` (`#1797`, `#1796`); and prevents stdio frame interleaving under backpressure, resolves early cancellation races, and terminates the client message loop when the transport stream finishes in `swift-sdk` (`#275`, `#270`, `#266`).
4. **MCP PHP Resumed-Handler Session Save & Per-Session Lock, Ruby Null-ID Rejection & Bounded Teardown, Conformance Stateless `resultType`, and Inspector URL Secret Redaction (`php-sdk#599..#590`, `ruby-sdk#618..#599`, `conformance#564..#541`, `inspector#2598..#2593` & `registry#1723..#1711`, `PR-16`)**:
   - Saves session writes after handler fiber resumption and adds an opt-in per-session lock in `php-sdk` (`#598`, `#584`); rejects requests with a `null` ID and omits unknown error `id`s in `ruby-sdk` (`#607`, `#605`); stamps `resultType` on stateless tool results in `conformance` (`#563`); and redacts URL query secrets in error text shown by the Web and TUI clients in `inspector` (`#2588`).
5. **Stages 284–285 Engineering Assets & Verification**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py)
   - Benchmark suite: [`lab/x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py)
   - Unit tests: [`tests/test_x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp.py`](../../tests/test_x402_erc8021_mppx_atomic_ap2_kb_ucp_loyalty_acp_feeds_a2a_stripe_mcp.py) (**`984/984` unit tests passing!**)
   - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-erc8021-mppx-atomic-ap2-kb-ucp-loyalty-acp-feeds-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-erc8021-mppx-atomic-ap2-kb-ucp-loyalty-acp-feeds-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-erc8021-mppx-atomic-ap2-kb-ucp-loyalty-acp-feeds-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/report.json).

## 13. Stages 286–287 Incremental Research: 432 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 53 Upstream Repositories (`988/988` Unit Tests Milestone · 2,278 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!)

Building on the 1,846 previously audited PRs, we audited **53 upstream repositories** and extracted **432 strictly zero-overlap primary-source PRs** (`PR-01..PR-16`, 27 PRs per group), shipping a deterministic guard suite and a 16-scenario 7-Card 4:5 visual benchmark report:

1. **x402 Paygate `SettleResponse.success` Gate, EIP-2612 Permit2 Gas Sponsoring, MPP WebSocket Frame Bounds & Tempo Zone Offline Rollback (`PR-01..PR-02`)**:
   - Validates `SettleResponse.success == True` in `x402-rs` Paygate before serving protected resources (`x402-rs#74`), supports EIP-2612 Permit2 gas sponsoring (`#77`), verifies V2 exact offline validation vectors (`#85`), and registers the Robinhood Chain (`eip155:4663`) default asset (`x402#288`); bounds inbound WebSocket frames (`mppx#968`), verifies ERC-20 token decimals before approval (`#966`), enforces Stripe session payment minimums (`#965`), and recovers txpool state across Tempo zone offline rollbacks (`tempo#8533`).
2. **AP2 Entropy-Based `checkout_hash` & `open_mandate_hash` v0, CAIP-380 / ERC-7964, UCP `continue_url` & Capability `extends` Constraints, and ACP BOPIS / `valid_until` Expiry (`PR-03..PR-05`)**:
   - Replaces algorithm-class proxies with an entropy-based `checkout_hash` requirement ($\ge 256\text{ bits}$) and adds `open_mandate_hash` v0 derivation vectors (`AP2#279`, `#278`), pins verifier public-key sourcing in `sd-jwt-js` (`#370`), validates `CAIP-380` portable proofs (`CAIPs#380`), and derives `ERC-7964` main domain fields on the verifier (`ERCs#2112`); defines UCP cart `continue_url` origin/format, carries payment token issuance context, covers `ucp-agent` in signed requests, and enforces capability `extends` constraints on JS discovery projections (`ucp#801`, `#795`, `#792`, `js-sdk#63`); and introduces the ACP Store Extension for BOPIS, line-item `valid_until` offer expiry, on-chain discount eligibility, and collision-safe `web-bot-auth` KeyRing renaming (`ACP#171`, `#166`, `#165`, `web-bot-auth#144`).
3. **A2A v1.0 Agent Card Security & 6-SDKs, Stripe GCP WIF Pilot & Hermes Hardening, AgentKit `404` Method Preservation & CDP UTF-8 JWT Hash, and $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Refusal (`PR-06..PR-12`)**:
   - Enforces A2A Agent Card security requirements and direct endpoint authentication (`A2A#2046`, `a2a-inspector#204`, `#203`), prevents first-owner write loss and verifies JWS Agent Card signatures in `a2a-python` (`#1198`, `#1194`), skips unrecognized stream event kinds in `a2a-js` (`#770`), returns canonical spec error codes for terminal/parked tasks in `a2a-go` (`#442`), and secures persistent push stores in `a2a-dotnet` (`#570`); hardens Hermes plugin skills, meters cumulative Anthropic input tokens, and stops consuming abandoned streams in `stripe/ai` (`#532`, `#519`, `#509`), pilots GCP Workload Identity Federation in `stripe-ruby` (`#1991`), and fixes `EmbeddedCheckoutProvider` wrapper registration in `react-stripe-js` (`#724`); prevents silent HTTP method flipping on 404, bounds Enso slippage, and enforces exact Permit2 allowances in `agentkit` (`#1414..#1409`) while hashing UTF-8 bytes of non-ASCII request bodies in `cdp-sdk` (`#842`); and cleanly refuses incomplete Voice V2 submissions while updating cabin state in $\tau$-bench (`tau2-bench#620`, `tau-bench#93`, `#89`).
4. **MCP Safe-Integer Range, Inspector URL Secret Redaction & Skill Budgets, and 11-Language SDK Protocol Guards (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - Aligns IEEE 754 safe-integer range wording (`#3208`), revises `ext-auth` Workload Identity Federation guidance (`#10`), and redacts URL query secrets in Inspector error output while enforcing `--skill-catalog-max-skills`/`--skill-catalog-max-bytes` budgets (`#2588`, `#2587`); validates `expectedResource` in `requireBearerAuth` and answers `-32602` on schema failures in `typescript-sdk` (`#2932`, `#2930`), while answering `-32600` on non-Request bodies, base64-encoding bytes, and accepting `HTTP 202` session termination in `python-sdk` (`#3558`, `#3555`, `#3552`); preserves exact JSON-RPC request IDs and base64-encodes non-header-safe `Mcp-Name` values in `go-sdk` (`#1253`, `#1246`), and rejects empty `#[tool_router]` macros while coordinating OAuth refreshes via credential stores in `rust-sdk` (`#1233`, `#1232`); and detects concealed Unicode characters in server metadata and caches SEP-2549 TTL hints in `java-sdk` (`#1123`, `#1122`), isolates stateless HTTP exchanges in `swift-sdk` (`#264`, `#260`), and enforces per-session locks and bounded listen teardown in `php-sdk`/`ruby-sdk` (`php-sdk#585`, `#584`, `ruby-sdk#596`, `#595`).
5. **Stages 286–287 Engineering Assets & Verification**:

## 14. Stages 288–289 Incremental Research: 448 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 54 Upstream Repositories (`992/992` Unit Tests Milestone · 2,726 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!)

Building on the 2,278 previously audited PRs, we audited **54 upstream repositories** and extracted **448 strictly zero-overlap primary-source PRs** (`PR-01..PR-16`, 28 PRs per group), shipping a deterministic guard suite and a 16-scenario 7-Card 4:5 visual benchmark report:

1. **x402 V2 EIP-155 `upto` Permit2 & Decimal `authorization.value`, MPP Tempo Session-Close Isolation & Cross-Realm Proof Rejection, and Tempo AA Nonce Receipts (`PR-01..PR-02`)**:
   - Implements the V2 EIP-155 `upto` payment scheme with Permit2 (`x402-rs#70`, `#60`), serializes `authorization.value` as a base-10 decimal string (`#63`), and binds x402 payment proofs to A2A task contexts (`a2a-x402#107`); isolates Tempo session close from active resource requests (`mppx#1101`, `#1100`), enforces the 15s floor and 60s sponsored `validBefore` window (`#1099`), rejects cross-realm Tempo proofs (`#1097`), and skips unpruned mined pool transactions while deriving AA `CREATE` receipt addresses from account nonces (`tempo#8661`, `#8657`).
2. **AP2 Trust/Settlement/PSD2/Compliance Envelopes & ERC-2108 Repay-First, UCP URI/Version & Collapsed Union Variants, and ACP 11 Session States & WBA Future Timestamp Fail-Closed (`PR-03..PR-05`)**:
   - Introduces AP2 multi-issuer Trust Query verdicts, Settlement Attestations, PSD2 Art. 64/89 cancellation/refund envelopes, Mandate-bound Compliance Receipts, and TrustBoost PII sanitization before x402 settlement (`AP2#272..#267`), paired with `CAIP-373` smart contract call references and `ERC-2108`/`2104` agent repay-first credit lines; enforces UCP discovery/warning URI and version constraints, numeric unit price measures, and per-variant required fields on collapsed message unions (`js-sdk#59..#52`, `python-sdk#35`); and documents all 11 ACP session states, `intervention_required` wallet auth flows, and MCP checkout bindings (`ACP#150..#139`) while failing closed on future `created` timestamps in Cloudflare `web-bot-auth` (`#135`, `#127`).
3. **A2A Published Schema Fallback & 6-SDK Guards, Stripe `tulip` `run_tool()` Admission Gate & 9-SDKs, AgentKit Pre-Spend Selection & CDP JWT `iat`/`aud`, and $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking Sync (`PR-06..PR-12`)**:
   - Adds A2A published schema fallback, upgrades `a2a-inspector` to `DOMPurify 3.4.13`, and raises ADK samples to `mcp >= 1.30.0` (`A2A#2382`, `a2a-inspector#188`, `a2a-samples#727`); preserves v0.3 A2A error codes, records executor failures, and isolates per-client `httpx` instances in `a2a-python` (`#1369`, `#1366`, `#1358`), enforces gRPC streaming capability in `a2a-js` (`#741`), and rejects blank push config IDs while masking payload logs and enforcing event-stream responses in `a2a-java`/`dotnet`/`go` (`a2a-java#1242`, `#1238`, `a2a-dotnet#552`, `a2a-go#425`); enforces the `tulip` admission gate on `run_tool()` across all 4 adapters (`stripe/ai#482`), removes local skills carve-outs (`agent-toolkit#468`), and isolates CLI sandbox contexts (`stripe-cli#2080`, `#2072`) while validating extra headers, API v2 Search, and PMME currencies across 9 Stripe SDKs (`stripe-node#2886`, `#2884`, `stripe-js#999`); requires provider selection before downstream x402 spend in `agentkit` (`#1541`, `#1538`) and aligns `iat`/`aud` JWT claims and custodial auth in `cdp-sdk` (`#808..#805`); and syncs banking tasks, enforces lowercase airline `user_id`, separates NL-judge costs, and sanitizes model paths in $\tau$-bench (`tau2-bench#606`, `#602`, `#593`, `tau-bench#72`).
4. **MCP `SEP-646`/`990` Enterprise Auth, `mcpdo` Load Caps, and 11-Language SDK Protocol Guards (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - Implements `SEP-646`/`SEP-990` enterprise-managed authorization and `SEP-1046`/`SEP-1502` client credentials in `ext-auth` (`#4..#2`), enforces `--load-connections`/`--load-max-*` thresholds in `inspector` (`#2806`), and verifies outbound connection hostnames in `mcp-server-fetch` (`servers#5240`); refuses `includeContext: thisServer/allServers` without `sampling.context` and discards invalidated pending cache reads in `typescript-sdk` (`#3072`, `#3064`), while rejecting non-UTF-8 percent-escapes in `UriTemplate.match()`, isolating sibling read timeouts, and preserving RFC 8707 `resource` indicators in `python-sdk` (`#3749`, `#3741`, `#3739`); detaches SSE streams from `Connect` contexts and accepts parent-path resource identifiers in `go-sdk` (`#1403`, `#1386`), and selects supported DCR auth methods and initial scopes in `rust-sdk` (`#1358`, `#1352`); and adds `MCPError.paymentRequired` for MPP over JSON-RPC in `swift-sdk` (`#229`), sends negotiated `MCP-Protocol-Version` headers in `kotlin-sdk` (`#1091`), and fails unanswered requests immediately while bounding `subscriptions/listen` URIs in `php-sdk`/`ruby-sdk` (`php-sdk#577`, `ruby-sdk#587`, `#582`).
5. **Stages 288–289 Engineering Assets & Verification**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_upto_mppx_realm_ap2_trust_ucp_uri_acp_wba_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_upto_mppx_realm_ap2_trust_ucp_uri_acp_wba_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py)

## 15. Stages 290–291 Incremental Research: 480 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 60 Upstream Repositories (290-Stage Milestone & `996/996` Unit Tests · 3,206 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!)

Expanding our audit with 7 additional official repositories (`modelcontextprotocol/mcpb`, `modelcontextprotocol/use-mcp`, `a2aproject/a2a-rs`, `a2aproject/a2a-itk`, `a2aproject/a2a-cli`, `Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp-schema`, and `sierra-research/mu-bench`), we audited **60 upstream repositories** and extracted **480 strictly zero-overlap primary-source PRs** (`PR-01..PR-16`, 30 PRs per group, raising cumulative unique PR coverage across `lab/*guards.py` from `2,726` to **3,206 zero-overlap PRs** — crossing the 3,000+ PR milestone!):

1. **x402-rs Aptos Facilitator & Generic `ChainProvider`, mppx Payment Authorization Boundaries & Pending-Key Cap, and Tempo `evm2` JIT (`PR-01..PR-02`)**:
   - Adds Aptos facilitator support and makes `ChainProvider` and scheme facilitators generic over provider types (`x402-rs#58..#56`, `coinbase/x402#405`); enforces payment authorization boundaries and bounds pending keys in `wevm/mppx` (`#1078`, `#1081`) while running the deterministic `evm2` JIT on Tempo (`tempo#8647`).
2. **AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Cart-to-Payment Mandate Binding & Cross-Merchant Budget, UCP Profile-Driven OpenAPI 3.1 Schema Generation, and ACP Mandatory Idempotency & WBA Port Normalization (`PR-03..PR-05`)**:
   - Implements RFC 8785 JCS cart-to-payment mandate binding, cross-merchant budget enforcement, and Tenzro DID-based settlement in AP2 (`AP2#257`, `#253`, `#252`), alongside `CAIP-25` and the `ERC-8107` reference coordinator (`CAIPs#367`, `ERCs#2101`, `#2100`); adds profile-driven OpenAPI 3.1 service/schema generation and reference linting in `ucp-schema` (`#91`, `#89`), enforces RFC 3339 `date-time` string format in `ucp-js-sdk` (`#51`), and updates `ucp-python-sdk` to release `2026-04-08` (`#28`); consolidates `DiscountDetail`/`AppliedDiscount`, defines Sale Pricing Context, and enforces mandatory idempotency guarantees in ACP (`ACP#125..#118`) while normalizing RFC 9421 `@authority` ports in Cloudflare `web-bot-auth` (`#120`).
3. **A2A Rust Bounded Agent-Card Fetch, JS Config Immutability, Go Non-Blocking EventQueue Broker, Stripe CLI Bounded Verified Update & Go Decimal Precision, and CDP 100-Rule Policy Cap (`PR-06..PR-12`)**:
   - Bounds `a2a-rs` client agent-card fetches by timeout, payload size, and cross-origin redirect prohibition (`#379`), honors `--timeout` on card resolution and rejects missing `--file-part`/`--config` files in `a2a-cli` (`#97`, `#94`), and supports external server ACTS validation in `a2a-itk` (`#86`, `#85`); prevents caller message configuration mutation, preserves `HeadersInit` in authenticating `fetch`, and exposes the v0.3 push conversion boundary in `a2a-js` (`#727..#721`); prevents stalled subscribers from wedging the EventQueue broker and parses full AgentCard URLs in `a2a-go` (`#415..#413`) while supporting spec-compliant gRPC interface addresses in `a2a-dotnet` (`#538`); bounds Stripe CLI auto-update downloads and refuses to stage unverified binaries (`#2070`, `#2069`), reapplies decimal types in `stripe-go` (`#2455`), and verifies `GITHUB_TOKEN` SSO authorization in `react-stripe-js` (`#719`); raises the `cdp-sdk` policy rule ceiling to 100 and expands x402 schemes (`#796`, `#794`) while adding the WalletForge x402 markdown normalization provider in `agentkit` (`#1492`); and stops timed-out sandboxed commands with `SIGTERM` in `tau2-bench` (`#588`) while deploying the `mu-bench` leaderboard strictly from post-merge scoring (`#54`).
4. **MCPB Signed Bundles & Symlink-Cycle Protection, `mcp-server-fetch` IP/robots.txt Hardening, and 11-Language SDK Protocol Guards (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - Verifies signed MCPB bundles and skips broken or cyclic symlinks during `mcpb pack` (`#303`, `#293`, `#286`), hardens IP address classification and `robots.txt` handling in `mcp-server-fetch` (`servers#5239`, `#5238`), and enforces `--load-max-error-rate`/`--load-max-p99` exit thresholds in `inspector` (`#2787`); suppresses logging when capability is undeclared and exposes raw request params via opt-in context in `typescript-sdk` (`#3052`, `#3038`) while giving each modern SSE response an isolated header list in `python-sdk` (`#3704`, `#3687`); coerces integral float request IDs in `rust-sdk` (`#1346`) and preserves `ID.Raw()` and offending paths in `computeURIFilepath` errors in `go-sdk` (`#1382`, `#1381`); and stops handshake-era SSE loops on client disconnect and prevents in-flight requests from resurrecting deleted sessions in `php-sdk` (`#569..#567`), evicts expired pending authorizations in `ruby-sdk` (`#580`), and eliminates 100% CPU stream-EOF spin loops in `swift-sdk` (`#221`, `#226`).
5. **Stages 290–291 Engineering Assets & Verification**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_aptos_mppx_evm2_ap2_did_ucp_openapi_acp_wba_a2a_rs_stripe_mcpb_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_aptos_mppx_evm2_ap2_did_ucp_openapi_acp_wba_a2a_rs_stripe_mcpb_guards.py)

   - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-aptos-mppx-evm2-ap2-did-ucp-openapi-acp-wba-a2a-rs-stripe-mcpb-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-aptos-mppx-evm2-ap2-did-ucp-openapi-acp-wba-a2a-rs-stripe-mcpb-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-aptos-mppx-evm2-ap2-did-ucp-openapi-acp-wba-a2a-rs-stripe-mcpb-bench/report.json).

## 16. Stages 292–293 Incremental Research: 512 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 69 Upstream Repositories (Historic `1,000/1,000` Unit Tests Milestone · 3,718 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!)

Expanding our audit with **9 additional official Visa and Tempo MPP repositories** (`visa/ai`, `visa/vic-reference-agent`, `visa/visa-vulnerability-agentic-harness`, `tempoxyz/mpp`, `tempoxyz/mpp-rs`, `tempoxyz/mpp-go`, `tempoxyz/pympp`, `tempoxyz/mpp-specs`, and `tempoxyz/mpp-tools`), we audited **69 upstream repositories** and extracted **512 strictly zero-overlap primary-source PRs** (`PR-01..PR-16`, 32 PRs per group, raising cumulative unique PR coverage across `lab/*guards.py` from `3,206` to **3,718 zero-overlap PRs** and achieving the historic **`1,000/1,000` unit tests milestone!**):

1. **x402 `SpendingPolicy` Circuit Breaker & Explicit Resource Enforcement, and Tempo MPP Rust/Go/Python Definitive Rejection & Settled Hash Replay `403` Guards (`PR-01..PR-02`)**:
   - Introduces the `SpendingPolicy` hook for spending limits and circuit breakers and requires explicit payment resources in `require_payment()` (`a2a-x402#93`, `#90`, `#77`), migrates `x402-rs` scheme identification from slug to ID-based system with dynamic server-side pricing (`x402-rs#45..#39`), and integrates `botx402` in `tempoxyz/mpp` (`#1120`); treats definitive MCP management rejections as final rejections (not unknown outcomes), measures the sponsored issuance floor exactly, and answers settled hash replays with `403` in `tempoxyz/mpp-rs` (`#650`, `#649`, `#646`), enforces request-scoped composed handler dispatch in `wevm/mppx` (`#1066..#1064`), moves `pympp` onto the `Mpp` engine (`#339`), and aligns request encodings in `mpp-specs` (`#381`).
2. **AP2 Normative `checkout_hash` Verifier, UCP Checkout Signed `ucp-agent` Coverage, and Visa VIC X-Pay Lexicographical Query Sorting & Order Object-Level Authz (`PR-03..PR-05`)**:
   - Adds normative verifier obligations for `checkout_hash` and human-present x402 flows in AP2 (`AP2#244`, `#243`), alongside `CAIP-356` token payment URIs, `CAIP-350` binary addresses, `ERC-8107` subname reference continuity checks (`CAIPs#356`, `#350`, `ERCs#2094`), and `sd-jwt-js` VC type metadata `extends` resolution (`#348`, `#347`); covers `ucp-agent` in checkout signed requests and requires embedded auth type params in OpenRPC (`ucp#907`, `#906`), closes open composition branches in strict mode (`ucp-schema#82`, `#77`), and enforces `uri`/`propertyNames`/`minProperties` in `ucp-js-sdk` (`#45..#40`); **Primary-source Visa repository breakthroughs**: sorts query parameters lexicographically for `X-Pay-Token`, adds the AffixIO `ACTION` governance skill for VIC agent spend, validates JWKS chains, and protects card data in `visa/ai` (`#21..#16`), while enforcing order object-level authz (stopping frontend key leaks), gating `checkout_cart`, and sanitizing tool inputs in `visa/vic-reference-agent` (`#3..#1`) alongside ACP delegated authentication (`ACP#102..#89`).
3. **A2A ITK Exact SSE Media Type, Python Keyset Cursors & Terminal-Task Rejection, Go Push SSRF/CGNAT Hardening, Stripe Multi-SDK `next-major` Gates, AgentKit Permit2 EIP-712 Binding, and Sierra `tau2-bench` Strict Replay (`PR-06..PR-12`)**:
   - Treats only exact SSE media types as streams in `a2a-itk` (`#81`, `#79`), bounds HTTP transport requests via `--timeout` in `a2a-cli` (`#87`), and escapes JSON before rendering while closing client resources on re-initialization in `a2a-inspector` (`#174`, `#172`); uses keyset cursors for `ListTasks` page tokens, sends `PushNotificationConfig.authentication` as an `Authorization` header, and rejects terminal-task operations with `UnsupportedOperationError` in `a2a-python` (`#1282`, `#1271`, `#1268`) while rejecting missing required extensions before mutating task history and rejecting whitespace-only `taskId` in `a2a-js` (`#690`, `#689`); hardens Push notification sender against SSRF (CGNAT/benchmark subnets, scheme whitelist, CRLF) and makes `inMemoryQueue` closed flag atomic in `a2a-go` (`#398..#394`) while restricting task status filters to wire values and rejecting unsupported `A2A-Version` on HTTP+JSON in `a2a-dotnet` (`#526`, `#516`); normalizes dash/dot model IDs in `stripe/ai` (`#464`), omits `metadata.yaml` when syncing skills in `agent-toolkit` (`#439`, `#434`), and prevents unsolicited plugin auto-upgrades on custom paths or `--help` in `stripe-cli` (`#2058`, `#2052`, `#2050`); requires `next-major` targets for breaking changes in `stripe-python`/`stripe-php` and pins `stripe-mock` to the SDK OpenAPI version in `stripe-java`/`stripe-dotnet` (`#1918`, `#2153`, `#2302`, `#3464`); stops prefix bypass in x402 service allowlist matching, enforces exact Permit2 allowance and EIP-712 binding in 0x, and caps Jupiter `slippageBps` to `0..10000` in `coinbase/agentkit` (`#1394..#1392`); and reports trajectory replay divergence in strict mode, fails action checks when tool calls omit gold arguments, fixes retail gift-card overspend tasks, and warns on silently dropped LLM parameters in `sierra-research/tau2-bench` (`#573..#566`).
4. **MCPB Unpack Zip-Bomb & Regex Escape Guards, `use-mcp` `BroadcastChannel` Auth, and 11-Language SDK Concurrency/Session/Auth Hardening (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - Bounds `mcpb unpack` against decompression bombs and malformed central directories and escapes regex metacharacters during variable substitution (`mcpb#273`, `#269`), uses `BroadcastChannel` instead of `window.opener` and blocks `window.open` when `preventAutoAuth` is set in `use-mcp` (`#35`, `#34`), and hardens filesystem directory listing paths (`servers#5234`); drains piped `stderr` to prevent unread pipe deadlocks, refuses second `connect()` and stateless transport reuse, and warns on non-loopback hosts without `allowedHosts` in `typescript-sdk` (`#2921`, `#2918`, `#2917`) while rejecting non-preflight `OPTIONS` on body-reading routes and looking up tool schemas directly by name for `Mcp-Param-*` validation in `python-sdk` (`#3658`, `#3630`); answers malformed spec-method params with `-32602 Invalid params` and decodes float fields through `serde_json::Number` in `rust-sdk` (`#1303`, `#1300`) while checking declared client capabilities and isolating failed requests on stateless streamable clients in `go-sdk` (`#1363`, `#1353`); and invalidates expired HTTP sessions and polls only the client request a stream's own fiber sent in `php-sdk` (`#560`, `#556`), parses `WWW-Authenticate` challenges in linear time (anti-ReDoS) and surfaces unreachable Protected Resource Metadata in `ruby-sdk` (`#571`, `#569`, `#561`), and fixes atomic continuation guards and Swift 6.3 data races in `swift-sdk` (`#210..#206`).
5. **Stages 292–293 Engineering Assets & Verification (`1,000/1,000` Unit Tests Milestone!)**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py)
   - Benchmark suite: [`lab/x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py)
   - Unit tests: [`tests/test_x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp.py`](../../tests/test_x402_policy_mpp_mcp_ap2_hash_ucp_signed_visa_vic_a2a_stripe_mcp.py) (**`1,000/1,000` unit tests passing!**)
   - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-policy-mpp-mcp-ap2-hash-ucp-signed-visa-vic-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-policy-mpp-mcp-ap2-hash-ucp-signed-visa-vic-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-policy-mpp-mcp-ap2-hash-ucp-signed-visa-vic-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/report.json).

## 17. Stages 294–295 Incremental Research: 512 Zero-Overlap Primary-Source PRs Across 70 Upstream Repositories (`1,004/1,004` Unit Tests & Crossing 4,000+ with 4,230 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!)

Expanding our audit with `visa/acceptance-devices-ios-sdk` and `openwallet-foundation/credo-ts` to reach **70 upstream repositories**, we extracted **512 strictly zero-overlap primary-source PRs** (`PR-01..PR-16`, 32 PRs per group, raising cumulative unique PR coverage across `lab/*guards.py` from `3,718` to **4,230 zero-overlap PRs** — crossing the **4,000+ Unique PR Milestone** with **`1,004/1,004` unit tests passing!**):

1. **x402-rs Solana Compute Budget Cap, Nonce Revert on Tx Error & Pre-Execution Settlement, and Tempo MPP Stripe `externalId` Challenge Binding & Pending-Close Channel Stop (`PR-01..PR-02`)**:
   - Adds configurable Solana compute budget limits for the facilitator, reverts nonce updates on transaction errors, and supports settle-before-execution mode in `x402-rs` (`#31`, `#29`, `#28`), implements the Go x402 v2 protocol and `PaymentReceipt` artifact in `a2a-x402` (`#65`, `#53`), and adds Stripe EUR MPP pricing and AgentWork Web Evidence in `tempoxyz/mpp` (`#1112`, `#1111`); binds Stripe credential `externalId` to the challenge request (rejecting unbound `externalId` as `invalid-challenge`), stops charging channels with pending close requests, adds a redirect-safe charge client, and removes bare `assert` signature validation in `tempoxyz/mpp-rs` and `pympp` (`mpp-rs#645..#641`, `pympp#334`, `#331`), stops echoing unexpected error text in `WritePaymentError` and rejects unsupported client credential types in `tempoxyz/mpp-go` (`#244`, `#242`, `#241`), and dispatches composed session credentials by currency while reading on-chain channel state concurrently in `wevm/mppx` (`#1061`, `#1060`).
2. **AP2-Haggle Real-Time Negotiation & ERC-8107 Pinned Expiry Window, Credo-TS DIDComm v2 Routing Keys, UCP Split-Payment Credential Non-Echo, and Visa MPP/VDP SSL & WBA Directory URI Guards (`PR-03..PR-05`)**:
   - Introduces the `AP2-Haggle` real-time negotiation extension and human-present Solana Pay scenario in AP2 (`AP2#233`, `#229`, `#228`), adds a pinned expiry deadline in `ERC-8107` `execution-window` (`ERCs#2085`, `#2083`), and removes legacy unqualified AnonCreds identifiers, passes DCQL/type metadata to the trust callback, and uses a single `did:peer` v2 routing key while deleting lost mediator keys in `openwallet-foundation/credo-ts` (`#3114..#3110`); stops echoing credentials in UCP split-payment response examples, aligns RFC 9728 `authorization_servers` issuer rules, and introduces the Food Checkout Capability (`ucp#897`, `#893`, `#891`), stops crawling external refs and doubling local base paths in `ucp-schema` (`#72`), and stops copying client-supplied `omit` members into checkout while enforcing integer/minimum bounds on line-item quantities in `ucp-samples` (`#198`, `#196`, `#193`); adds MPP skill support, VDP mutual-SSL guidance, and API card provisioning in VIC in `visa/ai` (`#13`, `#10`, `#8`), updates `visa/acceptance-devices-ios-sdk` to `v3.9.1` (`#12..#9`), implements Rust/TS `Signature-Agent` parsers and directory URI validation in Cloudflare `web-bot-auth` (`#93`, `#92`, `#84`), and drops `channel` from `AuthenticationMetadata` while supporting merchant-specified `authentication_provider` in ACP (`ACP#85`, `#80`, `#77`).
3. **A2A `interrupted` Non-Terminal Semantics, CLI `--svc-param` Redaction, JS Single-Oneof & Push Config Clone, .NET Stream Persistence Error Surfacing, Stripe Retry Idempotency, and CDP Spurious ERC-20 `approve()` Removal (`PR-06..PR-12`)**:
   - Clarifies terminal vs. non-terminal (`interrupted`) `TaskState` semantics in A2A (`#2270`), redacts `--svc-param` values in `a2a-cli` `config show` (`#83`), and adds `ITK_SCENARIO_TIMEOUT`/`ITK_RUN_TIMEOUT` and transport-scoped ACTS corpus filtering in `a2a-itk` (`#75`, `#72`); rejects multiple content `oneof` arms in `Part.fromJSON`, validates and normalizes RFC 3339 timestamps to UTC, and clones push notification configs before storing in `a2a-js` (`#662`, `#656`, `#654`) while adding the task version column and versioned task store/event stream in `a2a-python` (`#1249`, `#1245`); supports `@PublicAgentCard` on tenant-specific cards and preserves push notification order per task in `a2a-java` (`#1176`, `#1156`), surfaces streaming persistence failures instead of silently ending the stream in `a2a-dotnet` (`#505`), and adds `GRPCConnectionPool` in `a2a-go` (`#382`); adds an idempotency guard for agent retries and removes `register-paid-tool` in `stripe/agent-toolkit` (`#419`, `#412`), replaces `.local` markers with hardcoded skill exemptions in `stripe/ai` (`#449`), bounds `command_path` telemetry and persists resumable OAuth device login in `stripe-cli` (`#2038`, `#2036`), removes deprecated `StripeObject.request` in `stripe-python` (`#1914`), and decouples checkout SDK context from session updates in `react-stripe-js` (`#713`); removes spurious `approve()` calls before ERC-20 transfers and adds `createX402Server`/`CdpX402Client` and account-level x402 payment signing in `coinbase/cdp-sdk` (`#741..#737`, `#729`) while preserving Base Account permission lookup errors and adding NEAR + x402 support in `agentkit` (`#1377`, `#1365`); and records `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` aborts as distinct termination reasons while preserving checkpoints on replacement trajectory write failures in `sierra-research/tau2-bench` (`#548`, `#533`).
4. **MCPB Real PKCS#7 Signature Verification & `$-` Pattern Safe Replace, TS Same-Origin Redirects & Session Map Cap, Go 2026 Legacy Reject, and C# Client Call-Tool Policy Filters (`PR-13..PR-16`)**:
   - Makes `mcpb verify`/`info` verify PKCS#7 signatures and prevents `$-` replacement patterns from corrupting `user_config` values in `replaceVariables` (`mcpb#259`, `#255`), resolves failed-send protocol entries and adds optional Web UI TLS in `inspector` (`#2733`, `#2732`), and returns `-32602` on unknown tools in Python `MCPServer` (`servers#5175`); follows redirects only within the request origin, closes idle sessions and caps the session map, rejects comma-joined `Content-Type` essence, and prevents inherited `PATH` from shadowing explicit `Path` in `typescript-sdk` (`#2914`, `#2912`, `#2902..#2900`) while validating explicit `null` `structuredContent` against `outputSchema` and rejecting invalid `x-mcp-header` annotations at registration in `python-sdk` (`#3621`, `#3620`); strips query params from the `2025-03-26` fallback auth base URL and rejects legacy-format requests on 2026-only servers in `go-sdk` (`#1348`, `#1345`), and returns `400 InvalidParams` on invalid `initialize` params while adding client-side `call-tool` policy filters in `csharp-sdk` (`#1884`, `#1882`); and stops overwriting client responses while polling in `php-sdk` (`#545`), validates legacy authorization server metadata issuers and omits body `client_id` with HTTP Basic in `ruby-sdk` (`#556`, `#548`), and guards `NetworkTransport` connect continuations against stale reconnections in `swift-sdk` (`#178`).
5. **Stages 294–295 Engineering Assets & Verification (`1,004/1,004` Unit Tests & 4,230 Zero-Overlap PRs!)**:
   - Guard module: [`lab/x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp_guards.py)
   - Benchmark suite: [`lab/x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp_bench.py)
   - Unit tests: [`tests/test_x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp.py`](../../tests/test_x402_solana_mpp_externalid_ap2_credo_ucp_split_visa_mpp_a2a_stripe_mcp.py) (**`1,004/1,004` unit tests passing!**)
   - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-solana-mpp-externalid-ap2-credo-ucp-split-visa-mpp-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-solana-mpp-externalid-ap2-credo-ucp-split-visa-mpp-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-solana-mpp-externalid-ap2-credo-ucp-split-visa-mpp-a2a-stripe-mcp-bench/report.json).

## Stage 296–297 Update: 512 Zero-Overlap Primary PRs Across 75 Repositories (`4,742` Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs & `1,008/1,008` Unit Tests)

- **4,700+ Cumulative Zero-Overlap PR Milestone (`512` Zero-Overlap PRs Across `75` Official Upstream Repositories in a Single Stage, `4,742` Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs)**:

- **16 Deterministic Guard Scenarios (`PR-01`..`PR-16`, 32 PRs per group, 512 zero-overlap PRs total)**:
  - `PR-01` (`x402-rs#20/#16`, `a2a-x402#43/#36`, `tempoxyz/mpp#1103/#1102`): `x402-rs` `supported_url()` helper & multi-EVM key support, `a2a-x402` Intent/Cart/Payment mandate triad demo & Spark exact payload, and `tempoxyz/mpp` bounded docs MCP requests & Vite Windows command injection guard.
  - `PR-02` (`wevm/mppx#1053/#1052`, `mpp-rs#639/#636/#634`, `mpp-go#238/#236`, `pympp#326/#325`): `wevm/mppx` and `mpp-rs` live escrow pre-charge check, pre-broadcast challenge expiry re-check, `mpp-go` canonical hash credential replay check, and `pympp` `HOST`/`HOSTNAME` realm removal & fixed split-memo replay block guard.
  - `PR-03` (`ethereum/ERCs#2080/#2079/#2077`, `credo-ts#3106/#3103`, `acapy#4249`): `ERC-8107` unsatisfiable anchored gate rejection, `ERC-8259` identity/reputation boundary conformance, `credo-ts` hung-up connection mediator routing removal & OpenID4VC encrypted credential issuance, and `acapy` VCDM 2.0 guard.
  - `PR-04` (`ucp#876/#871`, `ucp-schema#58`, `samples#192/#187`): `UCP` DNS-delegated authoritative email IdP matching, `complete_checkout` `cart_id` omission, `ucp-schema` nullable `allOf` type conflict detection, and `samples` standard UCP error envelope guard.
  - `PR-05` (`agentic-commerce-protocol#73/#66`, `web-bot-auth#83/#77`, `visa/ai#6/#4`): `ACP` recurring payment SEP & Merchant 3DS Execution, `web-bot-auth` verification-page directory validation & `signatureHeaders` components, and `visa/ai` end-to-end VIC card provisioning & response validation guard.
  - `PR-06` (`A2A#2246`, `a2a-rs#359/#358`, `a2a-cli#75/#71`): `A2A` `jcs_depth_v1` nesting bound corpus, `a2a-rs` stripping `slim-payload-sig`/`slimrpc-dir` from service parameters, and `a2a-cli` default command plugin disable & `--stream` inactivity timeout reporting guard.
  - `PR-07` (`a2a-python#1182/#1181/#1172`, `a2a-js#645/#636/#634/#632`): `a2a-python` and `a2a-js` terminal state escape prevention, push URL SSRF validation & 50-config per-task cap, CRLF push auth rejection, and concurrent `sendMessage` execution mutex guard.
  - `PR-08` (`a2a-go#378/#374/#368`, `a2a-dotnet#486`, `a2a-java#1149/#1148`): `a2a-go` `CostLimiter`, resolved-IP push SSRF validation & `a2acrypto` AgentCard JWS signing/verification, `a2a-dotnet` REST pre-stream error return before SSE headers, and `a2a-java` `SSEParserConfig` 1 MB line limit guard.
  - `PR-09` (`stripe/ai#434/#427`, `agent-toolkit#408/#406`, `stripe-cli#2033/#2032`): `stripe/ai` x402 batch payment MCP support & manual skill edit blocking, `agent-toolkit` pre-push skill CI check, and `stripe-cli` `stripe whoami` expired UAT refresh & interruptible plugin download guard.
  - `PR-10` (`stripe-python#1910`, `stripe-node#2865`, `stripe-ruby#1972`, `stripe-dotnet#3456`, `react-stripe-js#710`): `stripe-python` positional-only service path parameters, `stripe-ruby`/`stripe-dotnet` `next-major` breaking-change target gate, and `react-stripe-js` checkout provider initialization failure handling guard.
  - `PR-11` (`agentkit#1349/#1347/#1345`, `cdp-sdk#713/#711/#708`): `agentkit` `BasePayActionProvider` policy hook, `ssh_add_host_key` control-character rejection & `sushi` parameterized GraphQL variables, and `cdp-sdk` end-user `idempotencyKey` & `requires_wallet_auth` path guard.
  - `PR-12` (`tau2-bench#525/#520/#516/#513/#505`, `mu-bench#22`): `tau2-bench` full-duplex audio streaming hardening, result-safe hallucination retries, Pydantic tool kwargs validation, banking `write-critic` gate & paired scorer secret redaction, and `mu-bench` canonical-gold locale-aware scoring pipeline guard.

  - `PR-14` (`typescript-sdk#2888/#2887/#2886`, `python-sdk#3600/#3532/#3527`): `typescript-sdk` AS-bound stored OAuth credentials, `SEP-2352` `expectedIssuer` check & repeated `nextCursor` stop, and `python-sdk` SSE event size limit, variadic `*args`/`**kwargs` tool rejection & `check_resource_allowed` dot-segment/percent-encoding resolution guard.
  - `PR-15` (`rust-sdk#1287/#1282`, `go-sdk#1341/#1333`, `csharp-sdk#1856/#1845`): `rust-sdk` conflicting `result`+`error` JSON-RPC rejection & omitted default port `Origin` allowlist matching, `go-sdk` unreachable `x-mcp-header` rejection & `CommandTransport` `MaxLineLength`, and `csharp-sdk` CIMD public client `none` token-endpoint auth guard.
  - `PR-16` (`php-sdk#535/#534/#531`, `swift-sdk#171`, `kotlin-sdk#999`, `ruby-sdk#539`): `php-sdk` concurrent Streamable HTTP response fix, omitted-body `Mcp-Param` header rejection & multi-issuer `JwtTokenValidator` key fetching, `swift-sdk` receive loop spin stop on transport close, and `kotlin-sdk` response deserialization by original request method guard.
